Warning: ADS-B simulates aircraft. Send it only into a dummy load or a shielded setup, never to an antenna.	警告：ADS-B 会模拟飞机。只能发送到假负载或屏蔽环境中，切勿接到天线。
Warning: AIS simulates vessels. Send it only into a dummy load or a shielded setup, never to an antenna: false ships can confuse real receivers, which is a safety risk and illegal in most places.	警告：AIS 会模拟船舶。只能发送到假负载或屏蔽环境中，切勿接到天线：虚假船舶可能干扰真实接收机，这有安全风险，且在大多数地方违法。

A higher rate shows more spectrum but needs more USB bandwidth and processor time: when the status bar reports samples lost on the radio side, choose a lower rate.	更高的采样率能显示更宽的频谱，但需要更多 USB 带宽和处理器时间：状态栏报告设备端丢失样本时，请选择更低的采样率。
A recording plays at the rate it was made at: set it with the file.	录制文件按录制时的采样率播放：请在文件处设置。
A scan is using the radio and sets its own rate.	扫描正在使用设备，并自行设置采样率。
DVB-T2 and DVB-T channels sit on this raster; a running radio may be opened again at another sample rate.	DVB-T2 和 DVB-T 频道位于此栅格上；正在运行的设备可能会以其他采样率重新打开。
Automatic: the width of the signal is measured in the spectrum and the channel bandwidth (5, 6, 7 or 8 MHz) is set for you.	自动：在频谱中测量信号宽度，并自动设置信道带宽（5、6、7 或 8 MHz）。
ISDB-T channel width: 6 MHz in Japan, Brazil and the rest of the Americas, 8 MHz in the Maldives (ISDB-T International).\nThe same signal, every time scaled by the width. Automatic measures it in the spectrum and tries each width\nwhile nothing locks; it opens the radio at 10 Msps.	ISDB-T 信道宽度：日本、巴西及美洲其他地区为 6 MHz，马尔代夫为 8 MHz（ISDB-T International）。\n信号相同，只是按宽度在时间上缩放。“自动”在频谱中测量，并在未锁定时逐一尝试各种宽度；\n此时设备以 10 Msps 打开。

ADC level (rms). %s\npeak %.2f   clip %.3f%%   DC %+.3f / %+.3f	ADC 电平（rms）。%s\n峰值 %.2f   削波 %.3f%%   直流 %+.3f / %+.3f
Bias-tee (antenna power)	馈电（天线供电）
Sends DC power up the antenna cable for an active antenna or LNA (e.g. a GNSS patch antenna). Leave it off with a passive antenna, and never with a DC short in the antenna path (some antennas, splitters or filters): it can damage the radio.	通过天线电缆向有源天线或 LNA（例如 GNSS 贴片天线）供直流电。无源天线请保持关闭；天线链路中有直流短路（某些天线、功分器或滤波器）时切勿开启，否则可能损坏设备。
Most GNSS antennas are active and need this on.	大多数 GNSS 天线是有源的，需要开启此项。
IQ correction	IQ 校正
Corrects I/Q gain and phase imbalance, which puts a faint mirror image of every signal on the other side of the centre.\nMeasured from the signal itself; now %.2f dB, %.1f degrees. Not applied in DTMB and analog TV (their signals fool the measurement).	校正 I/Q 增益与相位不平衡，该不平衡会在中心另一侧产生每个信号的微弱镜像。\n由信号本身测得；当前 %.2f dB，%.1f 度。DTMB 和模拟电视不使用（其信号会干扰测量）。
The spike in the middle of the spectrum is the radio's own oscillator leaking in (strong on the HackRF and RTL-SDR).\nRemove: filtered out of the samples (not in FM: a station tuned to the centre has its carrier there).\nTune beside it: the radio is tuned just beside the channel, so the spike falls outside it, and the channel is moved\nback digitally. Needs a higher sample rate (DAB about 3.5 Msps); a radio too slow for it removes the spike instead.\nA line that stays at a round frequency (e.g. 650.000 MHz) when you retune is interference from the radio's clock, not the DC spike.	频谱中间的尖峰是设备自身振荡器泄漏所致（HackRF 和 RTL-SDR 上很明显）。\n去除：从样本中滤掉（FM 中不用：调谐在中心的电台载波就在那里）。\n偏移调谐：设备调谐到信道旁边，使尖峰落在信道之外，再以数字方式把信道移回。\n需要更高的采样率（DAB 约 3.5 Msps）；设备太慢时改为去除尖峰。\n重新调谐时仍停在整数频率（例如 650.000 MHz）的线条是设备时钟的干扰，不是直流尖峰。
%+.2f MHz	%+.2f MHz
Which broadcast standard to decode.\nAuto alternates between DVB-T2 and DVB-T until one locks.	要解码的广播制式。\n“自动”在 DVB-T2 与 DVB-T 之间轮换，直到其中之一锁定。

margin %+.1f dB, FEC blocks decoded %.1f%% (last frames)	余量 %+.1f dB，FEC 块解码率 %.1f%%（最近几帧）
FEC blocks decoded in the last frame: %.1f%%	上一帧 FEC 块解码率：%.1f%%
0 dB margin is the edge of reception (25%); +6 dB or more is comfortable.	0 dB 余量是接收的边缘（25%）；+6 dB 及以上较为宽裕。
ATSC 3.0 does not report a signal-to-noise ratio yet; this bar shows how much of the data decodes.	ATSC 3.0 尚未报告信噪比；此条显示已解码数据的比例。
This bar shows how much of the recent data decodes.	此条显示最近数据的解码比例。
multipath: %s	多径：%s
Files and the test signal never lose samples: they wait for the receiver.	文件和测试信号不会丢失样本：它们会等待接收机。

Demod	解调
How the signal becomes the audio the decoders hear.\nAuto: USB below 30 MHz, NFM at and above.\nNFM: 12.5 kHz channel, no de-emphasis, 300 - 3000 Hz audio.\nISS SSTV on 145.800 MHz is FM.	信号如何变成解码器听到的音频。\n自动：30 MHz 以下用 USB，30 MHz 及以上用 NFM。\nNFM：12.5 kHz 信道，无去加重，音频 300 - 3000 Hz。\n国际空间站 145.800 MHz 的 SSTV 是 FM。
Sample stream. Green: the ADC level is in a healthy range. Amber: too low or high. Red: clipping.	样本流。绿色：ADC 电平正常。黄色：过低或过高。红色：削波。
DVB-T2: the P1 preamble symbol (frame start and mode) is being found.	DVB-T2：正在找到 P1 前导符号（帧起始与模式）。
Guard interval and symbol timing are locked.	保护间隔与符号定时已锁定。
DVB-T2: the L1 pre-signalling block (frame structure) decodes with a good CRC.	DVB-T2：L1 前置信令块（帧结构）解码且 CRC 正确。
DVB-T2: the L1 post-signalling block (PLP list, modulation) decodes with a good CRC.	DVB-T2：L1 后置信令块（PLP 列表、调制）解码且 CRC 正确。
A full T2 frame is being received and equalised.	正在接收并均衡完整的 T2 帧。
Forward error correction, inner code: share of blocks that decode.	前向纠错内码：可解码块的比例。
Forward error correction, outer code: share of blocks that decode.	前向纠错外码：可解码块的比例。
Transport stream: services found, continuity errors counted.	传输流：已找到的服务，统计连续性错误。
The player is decoding and showing pictures.	播放器正在解码并显示画面。
The player has audio buffered and playing.	播放器已缓冲并正在播放音频。
DVB-T: OFDM symbol sync from the cyclic prefix.	DVB-T：利用循环前缀进行 OFDM 符号同步。
DVB-T: the transmission parameter signalling bits are decoded.	DVB-T：传输参数信令位已解码。
Channel estimate from the pilots is valid.	由导频得到的信道估计有效。

click to tune to %.4f MHz	点击调谐到 %.4f MHz
Channel width: %s	信道宽度：%s
The width of the signal is measured in the spectrum and the receiver switches between 1.7, 5, 6, 7 and 8 MHz by itself.	在频谱中测量信号宽度，接收机自动在 1.7、5、6、7 和 8 MHz 之间切换。
The receiver's channel filter. Narrower keeps a strong neighbour out (150 kHz copes with one 10 dB stronger 150 kHz away); below about 180 kHz loud passages distort a little and RDS suffers first.	接收机的信道滤波器。越窄越能挡住强邻台（150 kHz 可应付相距 150 kHz、强 10 dB 的电台）；低于约 180 kHz 时响亮段落会略有失真，RDS 最先受影响。
The width is measured in the spectrum, and 6, 8 and 7 MHz are tried in turn while nothing locks.	在频谱中测量宽度，未锁定时依次尝试 6、8 和 7 MHz。
6 MHz in Japan and the Americas, 8 MHz in the Maldives (ISDB-T International). The signal is the same, scaled in time by the width.	日本和美洲为 6 MHz，马尔代夫为 8 MHz（ISDB-T International）。信号相同，只是按宽度在时间上缩放。
Follows the symbol rate: symbol rate x (1 + roll-off).	随符号率变化：符号率 x（1 + 滚降系数）。
Choose automatic or a manual symbol rate in the tuner to change it.	要更改，请在调谐中选择自动或手动符号率。

A second OnAir (Add network radio, rtl_tcp), SDR# or SDR++ connects to this computer's address and the port. Samples are sent as 8-bit IQ at the rate the radio runs at. Without 'allow remote control' the clients cannot change the frequency or gain.	第二个 OnAir（添加网络电台，rtl_tcp）、SDR# 或 SDR++ 可连接到这台计算机的地址和端口。采样以 8 位 IQ 格式按电台运行的速率发送。未勾选“允许远程控制”时，客户端无法更改频率或增益。
Sends the FT8, FT4, FT2, WSPR, JS8Call, PSK31 and CW stations you decode to pskreporter.info, so they show on its public map and statistics. A report is: the sender's callsign, frequency, SNR, mode, its locator when the message has one, and when it was heard; with your callsign, locator, antenna text and 'OnAir'. Reports are batched and sent every 5 minutes; the same station on the same band is sent once per 10 minutes. Nothing is sent until you tick the box. Spots are public: use your own valid amateur radio callsign. CW and PSK31 are only reported when the text says 'DE callsign'.	将您解码的 FT8、FT4、FT2、WSPR、JS8Call、PSK31 和 CW 电台发送到 pskreporter.info，使其显示在该网站的公开地图和统计中。一条报告包含：发送方呼号、频率、SNR、模式、消息中的网格定位（如有）以及接收时间；同时附带您的呼号、网格定位、天线说明和“OnAir”。报告会批量处理并每 5 分钟发送一次；同一波段上的同一电台每 10 分钟只发送一次。勾选复选框之前不会发送任何内容。Spot 是公开的：请使用您自己的有效业余无线电呼号。CW 和 PSK31 仅在文本中包含“DE 呼号”时才会上报。

The RX888 MkII has no program of its own in its USB chip: OnAir loads SDDC_FX3.img into RAM when the radio is in boot-loader mode (nothing is flashed to the radio). Official OnAir packages include this file; a copy built by hand may not.	RX888 MkII 的 USB 芯片本身没有程序：OnAir 在引导加载模式下将 SDDC_FX3.img 载入 RAM（不会刷写到电台）。官方 OnAir 安装包包含此文件；自行编译的副本可能没有。

listening on %s port %d, %d HTTP and %d WebSocket client(s), %d requests, %d refused	正在监听 %s，端口 %d，%d 个 HTTP 和 %d 个 WebSocket 客户端，%d 个请求，%d 个被拒绝
Programs on this computer need no token; any other address must send it as 'Authorization: Bearer <token>'. The API lists the radios, starts and stops the receiver, sets mode, frequency, sample rate and gain, and gives the telemetry and the spectrum as JSON, also as a WebSocket stream. Endpoints: see README.md.	本机上的程序无需令牌；其他任何地址都必须以 'Authorization: Bearer <令牌>' 发送令牌。该 API 可列出无线电设备、启动和停止接收机、设置模式、频率、采样率和增益，并以 JSON（也可通过 WebSocket 流）提供遥测数据和频谱。端点：见 README.md。
Stop optimising	停止优化
Optimise gain	优化增益
Optimise gain: steps through the radio's gain range and measures the reception quality at each step (the mode's SNR while it is locked, else the signal-to-noise ratio of the spectrum around the channel, else the ADC level), then sets the lowest gain that is within 0.5 dB of the best quality and does not overload the ADC. Takes about 40 s and needs a signal. The AGC is paused and stays off.	优化增益：逐步扫描无线电设备的增益范围，并在每一步测量接收质量（模式锁定时用其 SNR，否则用信道周围频谱的信噪比，再否则用 ADC 电平），然后设置与最佳质量相差不超过 0.5 dB 且不使 ADC 过载的最低增益。约需 40 秒，并且需要有信号。AGC 会暂停并保持关闭。
Undo gain	撤销增益
Go back to the gain (and the AGC setting) from before the last optimisation	恢复到上次优化之前的增益（和 AGC 设置）
Optimising gain %d/%d: %d dB ...	正在优化增益 %d/%d：%d dB ...
Every gain step overloaded the ADC: the lowest gain was kept	每个增益步进都使 ADC 过载：保留了最低增益
Gain optimised: %d dB (was %d dB)	增益已优化：%d dB（原为 %d dB）
judged by the mode's SNR	依据模式的 SNR 判定
judged by the SNR in the spectrum	依据频谱中的 SNR 判定
judged by the ADC level	依据 ADC 电平判定

Before you transmit	发射之前
Transmitting needs a licence or permission where you are. You are responsible for what you emit and for not interfering with others (including services that people rely on for safety). Connect an antenna, an attenuator or a dummy load to the radio before you start. OnAir does not limit the frequency or the power: the radio's own limits are the only ones.	在您所在的地方发射需要许可证或许可。您对所发射的内容以及不干扰他人（包括人们依赖其保障安全的服务）负责。开始前请将天线、衰减器或假负载连接到无线电。OnAir 不限制频率或功率：只有无线电自身的限制。

Receive only: OnAir never transmits, probes, joins or answers a network, and nothing is decrypted. The security type is what the beacon announces, shown for information. Sub-GHz Wi-Fi HaLow (802.11ah) is for low power sensors and cameras at long range; 1, 2, 4, 8 and 16 MHz channels. 1 and 2 MHz channels work from 2.4 Msps (an RTL-SDR), 4 MHz from 4 Msps, 8 MHz from 10 Msps, 16 MHz from 20 Msps.	仅接收：OnAir 绝不发射、探测、加入或应答任何网络，也不解密任何内容。安全类型只是信标所宣告的内容，仅供参考。亚 GHz 的 Wi-Fi HaLow (802.11ah) 用于低功耗、远距离的传感器和摄像头；信道宽度 1、2、4、8 和 16 MHz。1 和 2 MHz 信道从 2.4 Msps(RTL-SDR）起可用，4 MHz 从 4 Msps 起，8 MHz 从 10 Msps 起，16 MHz 从 20 Msps 起。

The data field is decoded for one spatial stream with BCC coding at MCS 0 - 10. With several streams (a radio with one antenna cannot separate them), STBC, LDPC coding (parity-check matrices not included) and A-MPDU the frame is detected and counted, its contents are not read.	数据字段仅对 BCC 编码、MCS 0 - 10 的单空间流解码。多流（单天线无线电无法分离）、STBC、LDPC(未包含校验矩阵）和 A-MPDU 的帧会被检测并计数，但不读取内容。

Receive filter width. 12.5 kHz cuts a neighbouring channel better but needs the signal on the channel centre; 25 kHz is the wider, more forgiving filter.	接收滤波器宽度。12.5 kHz 对相邻信道的抑制更好，但要求信号位于信道中心；25 kHz 更宽，容错更高。

RDS and the US RBDS share the data but name the programme types differently, and RBDS stations send call letters. Auto uses RBDS on the US channel raster (87.9, 88.1 ... 107.9 MHz); Europe has stations on those channels too, so pick RDS (Europe) there. Remove DC	去除直流
For WebSDR, a virtual audio cable (VAC), or a radio's speaker, line or IF output into the computer. Not a replacement for an SDR when you need megahertz of bandwidth. The frequency field is the dial the decoder should assume (the radio frequency of the IF centre); the sound card is not tuned.	用于 WebSDR、虚拟音频线（VAC），或电台的扬声器、线路或中频输出接入电脑。需要兆赫带宽时不能替代 SDR。频率栏是解码器应假定的刻度（中频中心的射频频率）；声卡不会被调谐。
I/Q stereo: left is I, right is Q (HDSDR / WebSDR VAC). USB audio: real audio to analytic IQ for FT8, RTTY, CW, SSB from a speaker tap or a receiver's audio. LSB audio: the same, mirrored. Real (mono): real samples (an AF tap, an IF such as 12 kHz) made analytic, with the sideband switch below.	I/Q 立体声：左为 I，右为 Q（HDSDR / WebSDR VAC）。USB 音频：将实数音频转为解析 IQ，用于来自扬声器抽头或接收机音频的 FT8、RTTY、CW、SSB。LSB 音频：相同，镜像。实数（单声道）：实数采样（音频抽头、12 kHz 等中频）转为解析信号，并配有下方的边带开关。
The audio frequency of the signal's centre in the sound card, for example 12000 for a radio's 12 kHz IF output, or 0 for plain audio. It is shifted down to 0 Hz, so the decoder sees the signal at the dial frequency above.	信号中心在声卡中的音频频率，例如电台 12 kHz 中频输出填 12000，普通音频填 0。它会被移到 0 Hz，使解码器在上方刻度频率处看到信号。
Mirror the spectrum: for a receiver whose output is the other sideband.	镜像频谱：用于输出为另一边带的接收机。
The sound card's sample rate, 8 to 192 kHz. A wider rate shows more of an IF. It applies when the receiver is started.	声卡采样率，8 至 192 kHz。更高的采样率可显示更多中频。在接收机启动时生效。
For WebSDR, a virtual audio cable (VAC), or a radio's speaker or line into the computer. Not a replacement for an SDR when you need megahertz of bandwidth.	用于 WebSDR、虚拟声卡线（VAC）或把电台扬声器/线路接入电脑。需要兆赫带宽时不能代替 SDR。

Use the mirror when GitHub cannot be reached	无法连接 GitHub 时使用镜像
Mirror address (https://host/{url} or a base address)	镜像地址（https://host/{url} 或基础地址）
A mirror is a third-party web address you chose. It sees that you check for updates and which version you run. It is used only after GitHub fails (not at all with GitHub only), the package is checked against the checksum from GitHub when GitHub can be reached, and nothing is installed from a mirror without your confirmation.	镜像是您自己选择的第三方网址。它能看到您在检查更新以及您使用的版本。只有在 GitHub 失败后才会使用（选择“仅 GitHub”时永不使用）；能连接 GitHub 时，安装包会按 GitHub 的校验和验证；未经您确认，不会从镜像安装任何内容。
The list of versions came through the mirror and GitHub could not be reached to confirm the checksum: only the mirror vouches for this package. Nothing is installed without your confirmation.	版本列表来自镜像，且无法连接 GitHub 确认校验和：只有镜像为此安装包担保。未经您确认不会安装任何内容。

The list came through the mirror; the checksum was confirmed by GitHub.	列表来自镜像；校验和已由 GitHub 确认。

Your radio cannot tune 225 kHz.	您的电台无法调谐到 225 kHz。
Long wave needs an SDRplay, an Airspy HF+, an RTL-SDR in direct sampling mode, or an upconverter in front of a radio that starts at a few MHz (set it under the radio's settings), and a long wire or a ferrite rod antenna. A BFO recording of the radio's audio also plays: tick Audio, not IQ in the Receiver tab.	长波需要 SDRplay、Airspy HF+、处于直接采样模式的 RTL-SDR，或者在起始频率为几 MHz 的电台前加装上变频器（在电台设置中设置），并配备长线或铁氧体棒天线。也可播放电台音频的 BFO 录音：在“接收机”标签页中勾选“音频”，而不是 IQ。

transmitter	发射机
computer clock	计算机时钟
ahead	超前
behind	落后
The computer clock when each time packet began, minus the stamp in the packet and the path delay, averaged over the last packets. It includes the radio's buffering (the earliest arrival is used). The frame timing of the service is from its description (GUM, v1.0) and not yet checked against a signal with a known clock: use it as an indication.	每个时间包开始时的计算机时钟，减去包内时间戳和路径延迟，对最近若干个包取平均。其中包含电台的缓冲（取最早到达的）。该业务的帧时序来自其说明 (GUM, v1.0），尚未与已知时钟的信号核对：仅作参考。
nothing to compare (a recording)	无可比较（录音文件）
once the time is decoded	解码时间后
path	路径
; set your position in ADS-B (Receiver position) for the right delay	；请在 ADS-B（接收机位置）中设置你的位置以获得正确的延迟
clock offset	时钟偏差
level	电平
start the receiver to see the signal	启动接收机以查看信号
carrier	载波
phase of the carrier	载波相位
The last two seconds of the carrier's phase, one point per 8 ms, newest brightest. The service keys the phase between two states 36 degrees apart (50 bit/s); a clean signal shows two tight clusters.	载波相位的最近两秒，每 8 ms 一个点，最新的最亮。该业务在相隔 36 度的两个状态之间键控相位（50 bit/s）；干净的信号呈现两个紧密的簇。
signal at the bit slicer	位判决器处的信号
The phase change over each 20 ms bit: a step of +-1 is a bit that changes the keying state, 0 one that does not; the slicer decides at half (dashed). The last two seconds.	每个 20 ms 比特上的相位变化：±1 的步进表示改变键控状态的比特，0 表示不改变；判决器在一半处判决（虚线）。最近两秒。
Frame	帧
Result	结果
no frame yet: a time packet comes every few slots of the minute frame	尚无帧：时间数据包每隔几个分钟帧时隙出现一次
, audio recording	，音频录音
Recording of the radio's audio	电台音频录音
Audio, not IQ	音频，非 IQ
The file is a recording of a receiver's audio (SSB / CW) tuned a little off 225 kHz, so the carrier is a tone (a WAV with the audio on the left channel and silence on the right; a mono WAV: eczastool rx reads it directly). Not for IQ files or radios.	该文件是接收机音频 (SSB / CW) 的录音，调谐在 225 kHz 稍偏处，因此载波是一个音调（左声道为音频、右声道为静音的 WAV；单声道 WAV：eczastool rx 可直接读取）。不适用于 IQ 文件或电台。
carrier tone Hz	载波音调 Hz
The pitch of the carrier in the recording: 1000 Hz when the radio's dial was 224 kHz (upper sideband).	录音中载波的音高：电台刻度为 224 kHz（上边带）时为 1000 Hz。
Not used: the Reed-Solomon bytes of the frame (a frame with a wrong check byte is dropped, not repaired).	未使用：帧的 Reed-Solomon 字节（校验字节错误的帧会被丢弃，不会被修复）。

Sync	同步
The test signal carries the computer's time with a time packet every 12 s: the clock offset should stay near zero (the simulator's own buffering shows in it).	测试信号每 12 秒携带一次计算机时间的时间包：时钟偏差应保持接近零（模拟器自身的缓冲会体现在其中）。

Predict positions	预测位置
Fills the gaps between position reports: the aircraft moves smoothly and, when messages stop, is dead-reckoned along its track (and along the route when a message named the destination). Predicted positions are drawn as a ghost with a dashed ring.	填补位置报告之间的空白：飞机平滑移动，消息中断后沿航迹（若消息给出了目的地，则沿航路）推算位置。预测位置以带虚线圈的虚影绘制。

The station, program data and pictures are shown, but HD Radio sound stays silent until you install the codec yourself. OnAir does not ship it (HDC is patented and owned by Xperi).	电台、节目数据和图片都会显示，但在您自己安装编解码器之前，HD Radio 的声音保持静音。OnAir 不包含它 (HDC 受专利保护，归 Xperi 所有）。

The noise floor is this percentile of the bin power in each hop (50 = median). Lower it in crowded bands, raise it on empty ones	底噪取每跳各频点功率的该百分位（50 = 中位数）。拥挤频段调低，空闲频段调高

WAV: 16-bit, large. FLAC: lossless, about half the size. MP3: small (needs libmp3lame). FM stereo is kept as stereo.	WAV：16 位，较大。FLAC：无损，约一半大小。MP3：较小（需要 libmp3lame）。FM 立体声保持立体声。

The SoapyRemote module is missing from this installation of OnAir, so it cannot reach a remote radio. Reinstall OnAir, and please report it if it happens again. The radio's computer must run SoapySDRServer --bind.	此 OnAir 安装缺少 SoapyRemote 模块，因此无法连接远程电台。请重新安装 OnAir。如再次出现，请反馈。电台所在的电脑必须运行 SoapySDRServer --bind。

Your radio cannot tune %.3f - %.3f MHz (the %s band); its range is %.6g - %.6g MHz. Use a radio that reaches it (HackRF, PlutoSDR, LimeSDR, BladeRF) or a converter set under the radio's settings.	你的电台无法调谐 %.3f - %.3f MHz（%s 频段）；其范围为 %.6g - %.6g MHz。请使用能覆盖该频段的电台（HackRF、PlutoSDR、LimeSDR、BladeRF），或在电台设置中设置变频器。
The nRSP-ST is reached through SDRconnect: run SDRconnect (or its headless build) on a computer with the WebSocket server on (port 5454) and its nRSP-ST selected, and type that computer's address. No password is needed or stored.	nRSP-ST 通过 SDRconnect 访问：在已开启 WebSocket 服务器（端口 5454）并选中 nRSP-ST 的电脑上运行 SDRconnect（或其无界面版本），然后输入该电脑的地址。无需密码，也不会保存密码。

Choose the radio or recording that supplies samples. Record IQ saves the raw signal for later playback.	选择提供采样的电台或录音。Record IQ 会保存原始信号，供以后回放。
Set the frequency, band and sample rate here. Gain and AGC set the input level; Second mode decodes another mode from the same capture.	在此设置频率、频段和采样率。增益和 AGC 决定输入电平；第二模式可从同一次采集中解码另一种模式。
%d modes, grouped by family. Use the search box to find one by name.	共 %d 种模式，按系列分组。在搜索框中可按名称查找。

Each mode has its own tabs for decoded output, settings and tools. They change with the selected mode.	每种模式都有各自的标签页，用于显示解码输出、设置和工具，并随所选模式变化。
Memory stores frequencies with their mode and settings. Frequencies and band plans list what is on air, per mode.	存储会保存频率及其模式和设置。频率与频段规划按模式列出正在广播的内容。
Each receiver pairs a radio with a mode. This strip switches between them and chooses which one plays sound.	每个接收机将一台电台与一种模式配对。此栏用于在它们之间切换，并选择由哪一个播放声音。
Listen plays the demodulated signal. Volume and mute are here; Record audio saves it to a file.	Listen 播放解调后的信号。音量和静音在此处；Record audio 会将声音保存为文件。
Server sends decoded positions to the public map. Support helps fund development.	服务器会把解码得到的位置发送到公共地图。支持有助于资助开发。

Calibration	校准
1. Feed all channels the same signal: the receiver's noise source (KrakenSDR: switch it on in the DAQ), or a signal from a known bearing. 2. Choose the matching mode and press Calibrate. The offsets between the channels are measured and removed.	1. 给所有通道同一信号：接收机的噪声源（KrakenSDR：在 DAQ 中打开），或来自已知方位的信号。2. 选择对应模式并点击校准。通道之间的差异会被测量并消除。

hidden	已隐藏
What LBJ carries	LBJ 传送的内容
LBJ is the Chinese railway train approach warning on about 821.2375 MHz. Every locomotive sends 1200 bit/s POCSAG numeric pages: a short message every few seconds with the train number, the speed (km/h) and the kilometre post, and longer messages with the locomotive type and number and the route.\n\nOnAir decodes the pages itself (receive only). A value that is blank had damaged digits; a row in grey had errors that were corrected, so check it against the next message of the same train.	LBJ 是中国铁路列车接近预警，频率约 821.2375 MHz。每台机车以 1200 bit/s 发送 POCSAG 数字寻呼：每隔几秒一条短报文，含车次、速度（km/h）和公里标；另有较长报文，含机车型号、机车号和线路。\n\nOnAir 自行解码这些寻呼（仅接收）。空白的值表示数字受损；灰色的行曾纠正过错误，请与同一列车的下一条报文核对。
tune to about 821.2375 MHz and start the receiver	调谐到约 821.2375 MHz 并启动接收机

Find KiwiSDR…	查找 KiwiSDR…
pick a public receiver from the list	从列表中选择公共接收机
A KiwiSDR is a receiver on the internet that covers 0 to 30 MHz; OnAir takes its 12 kHz IQ stream. Public ones have few user slots and many end a session after about 30 minutes: use one connection per receiver and leave when you are done. The password is kept in OnAir's settings.	KiwiSDR 是互联网上的接收机，覆盖 0 到 30 MHz；OnAir 使用它的 12 kHz IQ 流。公共接收机的用户名额很少，许多会在约 30 分钟后结束会话：每台接收机只用一个连接，用完请断开。密码保存在 OnAir 的设置中。

%s%.0f deg (average)	%s%.0f 度 (平均)
Reported by the aircraft.	由飞机报告。
Estimated by the position filter from the position reports (not broadcast by the aircraft).	由位置滤波器根据位置报告估算 (飞机并未广播）。
Derived from 2 positions %s apart: an average over that time; a curved path is longer than the straight line, so the real speed may be higher. The track is the average bearing.	由相隔 %s 的 2 个位置推算： 该时段内的平均值； 弯曲航迹比直线更长， 因此实际速度可能更高。航迹角为平均方位。
Derived from 2 positions %s apart (distance on the WGS-84 ellipsoid divided by the time). The aircraft did not report a speed.	由相隔 %s 的 2 个位置推算 (WGS-84 椭球面上的距离除以时间）。飞机未报告速度。

What this receiver has heard, by band: beacons (NCDXF/IARU, Long Dashes, single letters, carriers) and the FT8 / FT4 / FT2 / WSPR stations decoded in the HF digital screen. A band is open when at least 3 stations were heard in the last 30 minutes. Own receiver only: nothing is downloaded, so no solar flux or K index.	本接收机按波段听到的内容：信标（NCDXF/IARU、Long Dashes、单字母、载波）以及在 HF 数字界面解码的 FT8 / FT4 / FT2 / WSPR 电台。最近 30 分钟内至少听到 3 个电台即视为波段开放。仅使用自己的接收机：不下载任何数据，因此没有太阳通量和 K 指数。

Load a recording of the unknown signal, or paste packets you already have, and the lab works out how the signal is built: modulation, baud rate, preamble, CRC and fields. Nothing is decrypted.	加载未知信号的录音，或粘贴已有的数据包，实验室会推断信号的构成：调制方式、波特率、前导码、CRC 和各字段。不会解密任何内容。

One packet per line, as hex bytes or as bits. The packets are taken as sliced from the air: the preamble and the sync word may still be in them.	每行一个数据包，用十六进制字节或比特表示。数据包按从空中切出的原样处理：其中可能仍含前导码和同步字。

after a preamble	在前导码之后
The best reading is first. A check value that holds for most packets is strong evidence; without one, treat the rest as a guess.	最佳解读排在最前。若校验值对大多数数据包成立，就是有力的证据；否则其余只是猜测。

No check value found. Either the packets have none, it covers something this search does not try (for example header bytes in another order), the bits still hold errors, or the payload is whitened or encrypted. Try another demodulator, the other polarity or a cleaner recording.	未找到校验值。可能是数据包没有校验值，或它覆盖了本搜索未尝试的范围（例如顺序不同的头部字节），或比特中仍有错误，或载荷经过白化或加密。请尝试其他解调器、另一种极性或更干净的录音。

When all packets are the same length, the init value and the XOR-out value cannot be told apart: several pairs fit equally well and the first plain one is shown. A short check value (8 bit) with few packets can fit by chance; the more packets that agree, the better.	当所有数据包长度相同时，无法区分初始值和输出异或：多组取值同样吻合，这里显示第一组简单的。较短的校验值（8 位）在数据包很少时可能碰巧吻合；吻合的数据包越多越可靠。

Work out an unknown digital signal: modulation, baud rate, sync word, CRC and fields, with an export	推断未知数字信号的构成：调制、波特率、同步字、CRC 和字段，并可导出
Orbit from Doppler	由多普勒推算轨道
Follows the carrier of the tracked satellite during a pass and fits its orbit to the Doppler curve. Needs a steady frequency reference (a GPS-disciplined or well calibrated receiver); an error of the receiver's clock is fitted as a constant offset.	在过境期间跟踪所跟踪卫星的载波，并使其轨道拟合多普勒曲线。需要稳定的频率基准（GPS 驯服或校准良好的接收机）；接收机时钟误差作为恒定偏移一并拟合。
Measure the carrier during tracked passes	在跟踪的过境期间测量载波
gate +-%.0f kHz	窗口 +-%.0f kHz
How far from the predicted carrier the peak is looked for	在预测载波附近多大范围内寻找峰值
%d measurements of %s	%d 个测量值（%s)
Fit orbit	拟合轨道
Save refined TLE	保存精化后的 TLE
saved in %s	已保存到 %s
Writes the refined elements to a separate file, marked as derived from this measurement; the downloaded TLEs are not touched	把精化后的轨道根数写入单独的文件，并标明由本次测量导出；下载的 TLE 不受影响
Use the derived elements for predictions	用导出的轨道根数做预测
no measurements yet	还没有测量值
grey: predicted by the downloaded elements, blue: refined, green: measured, amber: residual of the refined curve	灰色：下载的轨道根数预测，蓝色：精化后，绿色：测量值，琥珀色：精化曲线的残差
residual %.1f Hz -> %.1f Hz (%d used, %d rejected); receiver offset %+.0f Hz	残差 %.1f Hz -> %.1f Hz(使用 %d，剔除 %d）；接收机偏移 %+.0f Hz

estimated timing error of the prediction: %.1f s before, %.1f s after (1 sigma)	预测的估计时间误差：之前 %.1f 秒，之后 %.1f 秒（1 西格玛)
Also share GPS interference cells (counts per area, no aircraft)	同时分享GPS干扰网格（按区域计数，不含飞机信息）
For every 0.5 degree square (about 55 km) around your ADS-B receiver: how many aircraft were heard in the last hour and how many of them reported a poor GPS position. Only these two numbers per square are sent, never aircraft identities, tracks or your location. It feeds the GPS interference layer of the public map and is an indicator, not proof of jamming. Off until you choose it.	对于ADS-B接收机周围每个0.5度方格（约55公里）：过去一小时听到多少架飞机，其中多少架报告了较差的GPS位置。每个方格只发送这两个数字，绝不发送飞机身份、航迹或您的位置。它用于公共地图的GPS干扰图层，只是迹象，不能证明存在干扰。在您选择之前保持关闭。

Keeps the last seconds of the radio's raw samples in memory, so the signal from just before something happened can be saved too	在内存中保留电台原始采样的最近几秒，这样事件发生前一刻的信号也能保存

Full rate	完整采样率
Samples kept	保留的采样
1 / 4 averages every 4 samples: the memory then reaches 4 times as far back, but only the middle quarter of the band is kept. The settings can be changed while it is off.	1 / 4 表示每 4 个采样取平均：内存可回溯 4 倍的时长，但只保留频带中间的四分之一。关闭时可更改这些设置。

One per line: an ICAO hex address (4840D6), a registration (A6-EDA) or a call sign (KLM1023). A trailing * matches the start (KLM*). Heard by ADS-B, ACARS, VDL2 and HFDL.	每行一项： ICAO 十六进制地址 (4840D6）、注册号 (A6-EDA) 或呼号 (KLM1023）。末尾的 * 匹配开头 (KLM*）。可通过 ADS-B、ACARS、VDL2 和 HFDL 收到。
One per line: an MMSI number, a ship name or a call sign. A trailing * matches the start (2190*). Heard by AIS and DSC. The entry DSC distress alerts on every DSC distress call.	每行一项： MMSI 号码、船名或呼号。末尾的 * 匹配开头 (2190*）。可通过 AIS 和 DSC 收到。条目 DSC distress 会在每次 DSC 遇险呼叫时发出提醒。
One per line: a call sign. Without an SSID (LY1ABC) every SSID matches; with one (LY1ABC-9) only that one.	每行一项： 呼号。不带 SSID (LY1ABC) 时匹配所有 SSID，带 SSID (LY1ABC-9) 时只匹配该 SSID。
One per line: a frequency in MHz (162.025), with a unit (162025 kHz) or a range (144-146). Anything decoded on it counts.	每行一项： 以 MHz 为单位的频率 (162.025）、带单位 (162025 kHz) 或范围 (144-146）。在该频率上解码的任何内容都会计入。
One per line: a word or a phrase (not case sensitive). A trailing * matches word starts (fire*). Searched in pager, ACARS, APRS and other decoded text.	每行一项： 一个词或短语 (不区分大小写）。末尾的 * 匹配词首 (fire*）。在寻呼机、ACARS、APRS 及其他解码文本中搜索。

Audio cleanup: noise reduction, notch, leveller ...	音频清理：降噪、陷波、电平均衡 ...
Audio cleanup	音频清理
Basic	基本
Settings for	设置对象
Automatic (%s)	自动 (%s)
A data mode: its sound is left as it is. Pick another kind above to clean it up anyway.	这是数据模式：其声音保持原样。如仍要清理，请在上方选择其他类型。
Clean the sound you hear. The decoders always get the untouched sound.	清理你听到的声音。解码器始终收到未处理的声音。
Strength	强度
noise reduction	降噪
auto notch	自动陷波
leveller	电平均衡
silence gate	静音门
voice emphasis	人声增强
Nothing is on for this kind of mode. See Advanced.	此类模式未开启任何功能。请参见“高级”。
Noise reduction	降噪
Takes down the steady hiss and rumble between and under the speech. More strength cleans more and costs some naturalness.	降低语音之间和语音之下持续的嘶声和低频噪声。强度越高越干净，但自然度会略有下降。
Auto notch	自动陷波
Finds a steady whistle, carrier or heterodyne in the sound and cuts it out narrowly, following it as it drifts. Lets go again when it stops.	在声音中找出稳定的啸叫、载波或外差音，窄带切除，并在其漂移时跟随。停止后即释放。
cutting %.0f Hz	正在切除 %.0f Hz
Manual notch	手动陷波
Cuts one frequency out of the sound, for a tone you can see in the audio spectrum.	从声音中切除一个频率，适用于在音频频谱中看到的单音。
width %.0f %%	宽度 %.0f %%
Leveller	电平均衡
Brings quiet and loud stations to the same loudness, with a soft limiter against peaks.	把弱台和强台调到相同响度，并用柔和限幅器抑制峰值。
balance %.0f %%	平衡 %.0f %%
target %.0f dB	目标 %.0f dB
Do not boost the noise between transmissions	不要放大发射间隙的噪声
Silence gate	静音门
Mutes the sound when only noise is left, like a squelch that follows the noise level by itself.	只剩噪声时将声音静音，类似能自动跟随噪声电平的静噪。
opens %.0f dB above noise	高于噪声 %.0f dB 时打开
muted	已静音
Voice emphasis	人声增强
Cuts the rumble below the voice and lifts the presence, so a weak voice is easier to understand.	切除人声下方的低频噪声并提升清晰度，让微弱的声音更容易听懂。
De-esser	去齿音
Softens sharp S sounds.	柔化尖锐的 S 音。

Let AGC turn the amp on/off by itself	允许 AGC 自行开关放大器
Off by default. With this off the AGC only moves the LNA and VGA and never touches the amp: it stays as you set it. The amp is the first stage and can be damaged by strong signals.	默认关闭。关闭时，AGC 只调整 LNA 和 VGA，绝不触碰放大器：它保持你设置的状态。放大器是第一级，强信号可能将其损坏。
This radio has no amp for the AGC to switch.	这台收音设备没有可供 AGC 切换的放大器。

The level of each of the 50 tones in the last second of the sub-audible band, left to right from 67.0 to 254.1 Hz. A tone that stands clear of the others for about half a second is taken as the CTCSS tone.	亚音频段最近一秒内50个音调各自的电平，从左到右为67.0至254.1 Hz。明显高于其他音调并持续约半秒的音调即被认定为CTCSS音。
start the receiver to see what is heard	启动接收机以查看收到的内容
Length	时长
Hear the channel (the sub-audible tones are taken out); with a gate set under Receiver the sound opens only for the chosen tone or code	收听该信道（亚音频音调会被滤除）；在“接收机”中设置门限后，只有选定的音调或代码出现时才有声音
sound card audio	声卡音频

Like the squelch of a radio with CTCSS or DCS: while the gate is closed the Listen sound is muted. The decoders keep running either way.	就像带CTCSS或DCS的电台的静噪：门限关闭时监听声音静音。解码器在两种情况下都会继续运行。
inverted	反相
DCS polarity (N or I) follows the deviation sign of the receiver; the same waveform can read as two codes, the second is shown as the alias. Five-tone tone lengths are vendor specific: when several standards fit, the one whose usual tone length is closest is listed first.	DCS极性（N或I）取决于接收机频偏的符号；同一波形可能被读成两个代码，第二个显示为别名。五音呼叫的音长因厂商而异：多个标准都符合时，通常音长最接近的排在最前。
Tone decoders	音调解码器
The test signal repeats every 40 s: CTCSS 123.0 Hz under speech-like noise, DCS D023N, DTMF 159#, a ZVEI1 call 12345, SELCAL AM-CP and a two-tone page.	测试信号每40秒重复一次：类语音噪声下的CTCSS 123.0 Hz、DCS D023N、DTMF 159#、ZVEI1呼叫12345、SELCAL AM-CP和一次双音呼叫。
CTCSS, DCS, DTMF, selcall	CTCSS、DCS、DTMF、选呼
A 406 MHz distress beacon (EPIRB on a boat, ELT in an aircraft, PLB carried by a person) sends a burst of about half a second every 50 seconds: an unmodulated carrier, then 112 or 144 bits at 400 bit/s. The message holds a 15 digit hex ID that is unique to the beacon, the country, the kind of beacon, and often a position (rounded to 15 minutes in a short message, to 4 seconds in a long one). Two BCH checks protect the message; up to three damaged bits in the first field are repaired.\n\nOnAir only receives. Test beacons and self-test bursts are marked. If you think you heard a real one, tell the rescue coordination centre of your country; do not rely on this program for safety.	406 MHz 遇险信标（船上的 EPIRB、飞机上的 ELT、个人携带的 PLB）大约每 50 秒发送一次约半秒的突发：先是未调制的载波，然后以 400 bit/s 发送 112 或 144 比特。报文包含信标独有的 15 位十六进制 ID、国家、信标类型，通常还有位置（短报文取整到 15 分，长报文取整到 4 秒）。报文由两个 BCH 校验保护；第一字段中最多三个损坏的比特会被修复。\n\nOnAir 只接收。测试信标和自检突发会被标出。如果您认为听到了真正的信标，请通知您所在国家的救援协调中心；请勿依赖本程序来保障安全。

MT63 comes in three widths, 500, 1000 and 2000 Hz (5, 10 and 20 characters per second). Automatic looks for all three.	MT63 有三种带宽:500、1000 和 2000 Hz(每秒 5、10 和 20 个字符）。自动模式会同时搜索这三种。

Short	短
The 64 carriers: the level of each, relative to the strongest	64 个载波：各自相对于最强者的电平
The long interleave spreads every character over 64 symbols, the short one over 32: the long one survives longer fades and bursts, the short one has less delay. Automatic decodes both and shows the one with clean codewords.	长交织把每个字符分散到 64 个符号，短交织分散到 32 个：长交织能承受更长的衰落和突发干扰，短交织延迟更小。自动模式同时解码两者，并显示码字干净的那个。

The beacon is a fixed transmitter far beyond the horizon: it is heard only when a meteor trail reflects it, as a short echo a few Hz to a few hundred Hz away from the line. Echoes closer than 18 Hz to the line are masked.	信标是远在地平线之外的固定发射机：只有流星余迹反射它时才能听到，表现为离谱线几 Hz 到几百 Hz 的短回波。距谱线不到 18 Hz 的回波会被屏蔽。

Voice inversion: undo a simple voice scrambler	语音倒频：还原简单的语音扰频
Voice inversion	语音倒频
Frequency inversion only mirrors the sound of a voice; it is not encryption. In many countries it may be illegal to listen to private communications. You are responsible for what you listen to and for following the law where you are. Encrypted traffic is never touched.	频率倒置只是把声音的频谱镜像翻转，并不是加密。在许多国家，收听私人通信可能是违法的。你需要对自己收听的内容以及遵守当地法律负责。加密的通信绝不会被处理。

Scan these channels with this radio	用此接收机扫描这些频道
Steps the radio through the channels: the distress channels are visited more often, and a locked decoder or a call keeps the radio on its channel.	让接收机依次扫描各频道：遇险频道更常被访问，解码器锁定或有呼叫时停留在该频道。
Decode the chosen MF/HF channels in the same capture	在同一采集中解码所选的 MF/HF 频道
Decodes every chosen channel that lies inside the radio's bandwidth at the same time (channels in a group are 0.5 kHz apart, the distress channels are MHz apart). Needs a sample rate of 250 kHz or more.	同时解码位于接收机带宽内的所有所选频道（同组频道相隔 0.5 kHz，遇险频道相隔数 MHz）。需要 250 kHz 以上的采样率。

none inside the capture	采集范围内没有

entries; ship names, call signs and types are learned from AIS (static data), and your own list can be imported.	条目；船名、呼号和类型从 AIS(静态数据）中学习，也可导入你自己的列表。

Copies the reports as CSV: id, time, platform address, channel, frequency, bit rate, coding, level, data format, parity errors, data.	将报告复制为CSV：编号、时间、平台地址、信道、频率、比特率、编码、电平、数据格式、奇偶校验错误、数据。
Appends every report to goes_dcs.csv in the data folder, also when nobody watches the screen.	把每份报告追加到数据文件夹中的goes_dcs.csv，即使没有人看屏幕也会记录。
How far above the noise a carrier line must rise to be followed. Lower finds weaker platforms and more false carriers.	载波谱线必须高出噪声多少才会被跟踪。调低可发现更弱的平台，但误报载波也更多。

No report yet. Tune the uplink band (401.7 to 402.1 MHz, centre 401.85 MHz) with 0.5 Msps or more: platforms send once every few minutes to once an hour, mostly at set times.	暂无报告。请以0.5 Msps或更高的采样率调谐上行频段（401.7至402.1 MHz，中心401.85 MHz）：平台每隔几分钟到每小时发送一次，多数在固定时刻。

The GOES and Meteosat data collection system: river gauges, weather stations, tide gauges and buoys send a short report on a 1.5 kHz channel of 401.7 to 402.1 MHz, at 100, 300 or 1200 bps. The 8-digit hex address names the platform. The receiver tries every bit rate and coding on every carrier it finds.	GOES和Meteosat数据收集系统：水位计、气象站、潮位计和浮标在401.7至402.1 MHz的1.5 kHz信道上以100、300或1200 bps发送简短报告。8位十六进制地址标识平台。接收机会对找到的每个载波尝试所有比特率和编码。
with a steady carrier on another channel	在另一信道上加入稳定载波
The test signal is three platforms on channels 60, 100 and 140 that each send a short report every 14 to 18 seconds.	测试信号为信道60、100和140上的三个平台，各自每14至18秒发送一份简短报告。
Platform reports, 401.85 MHz	平台报告，401.85 MHz
Codes	代码
start the receiver to see the codes	启动接收机以查看代码
Squawk and altitude, 1090 MHz	应答机编码与高度，1090 MHz
Mode A is the squawk, Mode C the altitude in 100 ft steps. A reply does not say which of the two it is: a code shows as A/C until it can only be a squawk or is matched to a Mode S aircraft by its squawk or its altitude.	A 模式是应答机编码（squawk），C 模式是以 100 英尺为步进的高度。应答本身不会说明是哪一种：代码显示为 A/C，直到它只可能是应答机编码，或按编码或高度与某架 Mode S 飞机匹配。
Receive only: item trackers are recognised from their public advertisements. OnAir never connects to one, sends to one or reads its owner's data. Identifiers stay in memory for 30 minutes after a tracker was last heard, are shown as the last two address bytes only and are never saved.	仅接收：物品追踪器通过其公开广播被识别。OnAir 从不连接追踪器、不向其发送任何内容，也不读取其所有者的数据。标识符在最后一次收到后仅在内存中保留 30 分钟，只显示地址的最后两个字节，且从不保存。

An alert is also sent to the watchlists: add the word tracker to a list of the Words kind to get a notification or a push message.	警报也会发送到监视列表：将 tracker 一词添加到“词语”类型的列表中，即可收到通知或推送消息。

A tracker that stays near a fixed receiver, such as a neighbour's tag, is reported too, and the levels are the radio's own, not calibrated distances. The detector is meant for a receiver that travels with you.	停留在固定接收机附近的追踪器（例如邻居的标签）同样会被报告，而且电平是电台自身的电平，并非校准过的距离。该检测器适用于随你移动的接收机。
Adverts	广播
Trackers advertise on all three channels in turn, so any one of them hears every tracker.	追踪器依次在三个信道上广播，因此任何一个信道都能收到所有追踪器。
Alert after	警报时间
How long a tracker must stay with the receiver before the alert.	追踪器在发出警报前必须与接收机相伴多久。
Near level	近距电平
The averaged level from which a tracker counts as near. The radio's level, not a calibrated distance.	追踪器被视为靠近的平均电平。这是电台的电平，不是校准过的距离。

Telemetry	遥测
start the receiver to see the beacon	启动接收机以查看信标
No narrow carrier found. Tune so that the beacon lies inside the capture: a Ku-band beacon seen through an LNB is usually within a few hundred kHz of the centre.	未找到窄带载波。请调谐使信标落在采集范围内：通过 LNB 接收的 Ku 波段信标通常位于中心附近几百 kHz 以内。
Appends the carrier measurements and every frame to satellite_beacon.csv in the data folder, also when nobody watches the screen.	把载波测量值和每一帧追加到数据文件夹中的 satellite_beacon.csv，即使无人查看屏幕也会记录。
Copies the learned frames as hex lines.	将已学习的帧复制为十六进制行。
capture spectrum (dB over the noise): the narrow lines are carriers	采集频谱（高于噪声的 dB）：窄线是载波
phase modulation of the carrier (dB over the floor): the telemetry tones stand out	载波的相位调制（高于底噪的 dB）：遥测音调十分突出
No telemetry: the carrier is not phase modulated, or the tones are too weak.	无遥测：载波未被相位调制，或音调太弱。

A satellite beacon is a carrier, sometimes phase modulated with telemetry, that ground stations use to measure the link. The carrier is followed in frequency and level; the telemetry is recovered as raw frames and the frame length is learned from their repetition. The layout of a given satellite's frames is not decoded.	卫星信标是一种载波，有时用遥测对其进行相位调制，地面站用它来测量链路。程序按频率和电平跟踪载波；遥测被恢复为原始帧，帧长度则根据帧的重复规律学习得到。特定卫星的帧结构不会被解码。
The test signal is a carrier 236 kHz off the centre, drifting like an LNB, with a second carrier 545 kHz above it and 4800 bit/s bi-phase telemetry on a 48 kHz subcarrier, in frames of 256 bytes.	测试信号是偏离中心 236 kHz、像 LNB 一样漂移的载波，其上方 545 kHz 处有第二个载波，并在 48 kHz 副载波上以双相码携带 4800 bit/s 的遥测，帧长 256 字节。
Carrier, drift, telemetry frames	载波、漂移、遥测帧
decoded	已解码
estimated	估计
unknown meaning	含义未知
decoded: checked against the data. estimated: inferred, hover for the reason. unknown meaning: shown as it is; the meaning of a word is the spacecraft maker's and is never guessed.	已解码：已对照数据核实。估计：由推断得出，悬停可看原因。含义未知：原样显示；字的含义由卫星制造商决定，绝不猜测。

The radio runs this many times faster than the mode asks, and OnAir filters the samples down to the mode's rate itself.\nFor radios that are poor at low sample rates (images, a weak anti-alias filter). Costs CPU and USB bandwidth;\nthe radio's highest rate still applies. Off: the radio runs at the mode's rate.	电台以模式所需速率的这一倍数运行，由 OnAir 自行把采样滤波到模式所需速率。\n适用于不擅长低采样率的电台（镜像、抗混叠滤波器差）。代价是 CPU 和 USB 带宽；\n仍受电台最高速率限制。关闭：电台以模式所需速率运行。
At a set speed each row is the highest level of the frames in between, so a short burst is never missed.\nAverage: the mean level instead, which calms the noise and shows steady weak signals better.	固定速度下，每行取帧之间的最高电平，短突发绝不会错过。\n平均：改为取平均电平，可压平噪声，更好地显示稳定的弱信号。

Saves the rows now in the table (with the search and the filters applied, built-in and yours) as a CSV file. Import and the Memory panel read it back.	将表中当前的行（应用搜索和筛选，内置和自己的）保存为 CSV 文件。导入和“记忆”面板可以读回。
Clean the input: DC spike, IQ imbalance and the radio's own spurs	清理输入：直流尖峰、IQ 不平衡和接收机自身的杂散
Measured from the signal itself, never the wanted channel: the DC spike and the IQ mirror image are skipped in the modes they would harm, and a spur inside the channel is cut only when it stays at the same place of the baseband after a retune (a real signal moves with the tuner).	根据信号本身测量，绝不影响所需信道：在会受其损害的模式中会跳过直流尖峰和 IQ 镜像，信道内的杂散只有在换频后仍停留在基带同一位置时才会被切除（真实信号会随调谐器移动）。

Which antenna will you use? It matters more than the radio. OnAir uses the answer for hints only; nothing is changed in the radio.	你会使用哪种天线?它比电台更重要。OnAir 只把答案用于提示，不会更改电台的任何设置。
What this means for you	这对你意味着什么
Digital voice (DMR, P25, NXDN, dPMR, D-STAR, Yaesu, TETRA) is silent until you install its codec plug-in. The calls, IDs and data work without it.	数字语音（DMR、P25、NXDN、dPMR、D-STAR、Yaesu、TETRA）在安装其编解码器插件之前是静音的。通话、ID 和数据无需它也能工作。
Only digital voice modes need a codec. You can skip this screen and come back from Help > Voice codecs.	只有数字语音模式需要编解码器。你可以跳过此屏幕，之后从 帮助 > 语音编解码器 回来。
OnAir does not include or download these codecs (they are patented). You build or install them on your own computer; whether you may use them is your own responsibility. FreeDV, M17, DAB, DRM and TV need nothing.	OnAir 不包含也不下载这些编解码器（它们受专利保护）。你在自己的电脑上构建或安装；能否使用由你自己负责。FreeDV、M17、DAB、DRM 和电视无需任何东西。
Antenna: %s	天线：%s
Easy mode (recommended)	简易模式（推荐)
Every mode opens its Simple view: the spectrum and one screen with a Start button and the key controls. The detailed tabs are one click away (Simple | Advanced above them).	每个模式都以简易视图打开：频谱，加一个带开始按钮和主要控件的屏幕。详细标签页一键可达（上方的 简易 | 高级）。
Change any of this at any time: Help > Setup and lessons > Run setup again, Mode > Modes shown, View > Language, Settings > Appearance > Font look.	以上内容随时可改：帮助 > 设置与课程 > 重新运行设置，模式 > 显示的模式，视图 > 语言，设置 > 外观 > 字体外观。
The setup has six short screens and every one can be skipped. Nothing is forced.\n\nRadio: OnAir looks for radios on USB and on the network and picks one. "?" beside a radio opens its help page. Without a radio, the test signal lets you try OnAir.\n\nLocation: one click on a region preset (Lithuania, the EU, ...) or a city, a locator or coordinates give your country, the band plan, the TV channels, the FM band and step, the default frequencies and the units.\n\nAntenna: hints for what you can hear with the antenna you have.\n\nInterests: decide which modes are starred and listed first, where OnAir starts and a few starter presets. Everything stays available.\n\nCodecs: digital voice needs a codec plug-in that you install yourself; Look again checks the folder.\n\nEasy mode on the last screen opens every mode in its Simple view. Run the setup again any time from Help > Setup and lessons > Run setup again, or from Settings.	设置共有六个简短的屏幕，每一个都可以跳过。没有任何强制项。\n\n电台：OnAir 通过 USB 和网络查找电台并选择一个。电台旁的“?”打开其帮助页面。没有电台时，测试信号可让你试用 OnAir。\n\n位置：点击一个地区预设（立陶宛、欧盟等），或输入城市、定位码或坐标，即可设置国家、频段规划、电视频道、调频频段和步进、默认频率和单位。\n\n天线：根据你的天线说明能听到什么。\n\n兴趣：决定哪些模式加星并排在前面、OnAir 从哪里开始，以及一些起始预设。所有内容仍然可用。\n\n编解码器：数字语音需要你自己安装的编解码器插件；再找一次会检查文件夹。\n\n最后一屏的简易模式会让每个模式都以简易视图打开。随时可从 帮助 > 设置与课程 > 重新运行设置 或设置中再次运行。

Why	原因
no signal at the channel: check the tuning, the antenna and the gain	信道中没有信号：请检查调谐、天线和增益
signal, but no frame sync found: check the tuning (offset, polarity), the bandwidth and the signal-to-noise ratio	有信号，但未找到帧同步：请检查调谐（偏移、极性）、带宽和信噪比
frame syncs found, but their identity (NID, LICH, slot type) is unreadable: the signal is too noisy or distorted	找到了帧同步，但其标识（NID、LICH、时隙类型）无法读取：信号噪声或失真过大
frame syncs found, but the payload fails its FEC / CRC: weak signal, fading or a sample clock error	找到了帧同步，但负载未通过 FEC / CRC：信号弱、衰落或采样时钟误差
decoding, but over a third of the blocks are lost: a stronger signal or a better antenna would help	正在解码，但超过三分之一的数据块丢失：更强的信号或更好的天线会有帮助
decoding well	解码良好

AI assistant (MCP)	AI 助手 (MCP)
Let an AI assistant control this receiver (MCP)	允许 AI 助手控制此接收机 (MCP)
An AI assistant (Claude, ChatGPT-style clients, a local model) that you connect can tune, scan bands, identify signals and start decoders for you, and read what was decoded. It cannot read files, run programs, change settings or decrypt anything, and it cannot transmit unless you allow that below. Every action is listed in the log.	您连接的 AI 助手 (Claude、类 ChatGPT 客户端、本地模型) 可以为您调谐、扫描频段、识别信号、启动解码器并读取解码结果。它不能读取文件、运行程序、更改设置或解密任何内容，除非您在下面允许，否则也不能发射。每个操作都会记录在日志中。
Programs on this computer need the token too	此电脑上的程序也需要令牌
Allow the AI to transmit (off by default; this is your decision, it is forgotten at restart)	允许 AI 发射 (默认关闭；由您决定，重启后会忘记)
The AI can now start transmissions. You are responsible for the law where you are and for your hardware.	AI 现在可以开始发射。您须对所在地的法律和您的设备负责。
listening on %s port %d, %d requests, %d refused	正在 %s 的端口 %d 上监听，%d 个请求，%d 个被拒绝

How to connect your AI	如何连接您的 AI
Other client	其他客户端
Claude Desktop: put this in its config file (Settings > Developer > Edit config) and restart it; it needs Node.js for the bridge. Claude Code: run the command in a terminal. Other clients: use the address (streamable HTTP) or start onair-server --mcp-stdio. Then ask, for example: there is some DRM around 15 MHz, find it and decode it.	Claude Desktop：把它放入其配置文件 (设置 > 开发者 > 编辑配置) 并重启；桥接需要 Node.js。Claude Code：在终端中运行该命令。其他客户端：使用该地址 (可流式 HTTP) 或启动 onair-server --mcp-stdio。然后可以这样问：15 MHz 附近有 DRM，找到并解码它。

Start the receiver to begin.	请先启动接收机。
The decoders' share of one processor core, against the limit. Above the limit the lowest ranked signals are paused.	解码器占用单个处理器核心的比例与上限的对比。超过上限时，排名最低的信号会被暂停。
Find every signal in the whole capture (digital voice, TETRA, pagers, packet radio, air band, Inmarsat, TV) and decode all of them at once.	查找整个捕获中的所有信号（数字语音、TETRA、寻呼机、分组无线电、航空频段、Inmarsat、电视），并同时解码。
One capture, every signal in it. OnAir finds the signals, says what each one is and starts the right decoder for it. Switch one off, pin the ones that must never be paused, or give an unknown signal its mode by hand.	一次捕获，其中所有信号。OnAir 会找到信号、识别其类型并启动合适的解码器。可关闭某个信号、固定不可暂停的信号，或手动为未知信号指定模式。
When the decoders take longer than this share of one core, the lowest ranked signal is paused (never a pinned one or the one you listen to) and comes back when there is room. 0: no limit.	当解码器占用超过单个核心的这一比例时，排名最低的信号会被暂停（固定的信号和正在收听的信号除外），有余量时恢复。0：不限制。
This computer cannot keep up: %d signals are paused to save CPU. Pin the ones you need, or raise the limit.	这台电脑跟不上：为节省 CPU，已暂停 %d 个信号。请固定需要的信号，或提高上限。
TV channels in the capture: %.0f MHz wide	捕获中的电视频道：带宽 %.0f MHz

One radio listens to the control channel of a trunked system. When it announces a call, another radio is tuned to the voice channel, and the call is logged. With one radio, calls inside its capture are followed and the rest are not.	一台接收机监听集群系统的控制信道。有通话通知时，另一台接收机调谐到语音信道并记录通话。只有一台时，仅跟踪其采集带宽内的通话。

Tunes a span that holds the satellites about to pass, follows their Doppler shift, finds every narrow carrier, works out its modulation and baud rate, and decodes it with every framing that fits. Nothing to set up.	调谐到包含即将过境卫星的频段，跟踪多普勒频移，找出每个窄带载波，判断其调制方式和波特率，并用所有可能的帧格式解码。无需任何设置。

Where transmitter positions come from	发射台位置的来源
A DAB transmitter sends only its own TII code (MainId / SubId), not where it stands. Some networks also send a table of positions (FIG 0/22), most do not: then a list is needed that says where each code of the ensemble stands.\n\n1. The shared list (OnAir-channels, public domain): filled by users. It is downloaded for the country set below and for the country of every ensemble heard. A country nobody has shared a transmitter for has no list yet.\n\n2. qt-dab's list: if you use qt-dab, its file .txdata.tii in your home folder (or helpers/tiiFile.zip from the qt-dab repository, unzipped) can be used with "import list..." or the button OnAir shows when it finds the file. It holds FMLIST's data, which is for personal use: OnAir reads it where it is, does not copy, export or share it, and does not ship it.\n\n3. Your own file (dab-transmitters.csv, "open folder"): CSV, columns eid,main,sub,lat,lon,site,power_kw,channel_mhz. "export heard" writes every transmitter heard in these columns: fill in the positions you know and "import list..." it back.\n\nOnAir does not ship a list for Germany or other countries where no list with a licence that allows it exists (FMLIST, ukwtv.de and wohnort.org do not allow copying; the Bundesnetzagentur's data has no TII codes). Sharing a transmitter you know ("share...") is optional and makes it work for everyone. See docs/dab-transmitters.md.	DAB 发射台只发送自己的 TII 代码（MainId / SubId），不发送所在位置。有些网络同时发送位置表（FIG 0/22），大多数不发送：这时就需要一份列表来说明该节目集的每个代码位于何处。\n\n1. 共享列表（OnAir-channels，公有领域）：由用户填写。会为您在下方设置的国家以及听到的每个节目集所在国家下载。没有人共享过发射台的国家尚无列表。\n\n2. qt-dab 的列表：如果您使用 qt-dab，其主文件夹中的 .txdata.tii 文件（或 qt-dab 仓库中的 helpers/tiiFile.zip，解压后）可通过“导入列表...”或 OnAir 找到该文件时显示的按钮使用。其中是 FMLIST 的数据，仅限个人使用：OnAir 在其所在位置读取，不复制、不导出、不共享，也不随附。\n\n3. 您自己的文件（dab-transmitters.csv，“打开文件夹”）：CSV，列为 eid,main,sub,lat,lon,site,power_kw,channel_mhz。“导出已听到的”会按这些列写出听到的每个发射台：填上您知道的位置后再用“导入列表...”读回。\n\n对于德国等没有许可允许共享的列表的国家（FMLIST、ukwtv.de 和 wohnort.org 不允许复制；德国联邦网络管理局的数据没有 TII 代码），OnAir 不随附列表。共享您知道的发射台（“共享...”）是可选的，但会让它对所有人可用。见 docs/dab-transmitters.md。

Analog voice scrambler, full-band inversion (every audio frequency f becomes fc - f). Tune a narrow-FM voice channel. The long-term spectrum of the voiced audio is compared with speech as heard and flipped about each carrier from 2.7 to 3.7 kHz; the carrier is found to about 100 - 200 Hz. Split-band inversion and rolling-code scramblers are not covered.	模拟语音扰频器，全频段倒频（每个音频频率 f 变为 fc - f）。调谐到窄带调频语音信道。把语音音频的长期频谱与听到的语音相比较，并围绕 2.7 至 3.7 kHz 的每个载波翻转；载波可精确到约 100 - 200 Hz。不包括分段倒频和滚动码扰频器。
HF packet radio, full decode: AX.25 frames in 300 Bd AFSK, tones 1600 / 1800 Hz (200 Hz shift), upper sideband. Every frame with a good CRC is shown as sender>destination,path:text; a tuning error of up to a quarter of the shift is followed.	短波分组无线电，完整解码：300 Bd AFSK 中的 AX.25 帧，音调 1600 / 1800 Hz（频移 200 Hz），上边带。每个 CRC 正确的帧显示为 发送方>目的地，路径：文本；频调误差在频移的四分之一以内都能跟随。
Emergency locator transmitter on 121.5 MHz, detection only: an AM carrier with a tone that sweeps downwards from about 1600 to 300 Hz two to four times a second. The sweep, its rate and its depth are measured; the old analogue beacons carry no data. Tune the AM channel.	121.5 MHz 紧急定位发射机，仅检测：调幅载波，带一个每秒向下扫描 2 至 4 次、从约 1600 Hz 到 300 Hz 的音调。会测量扫描的速率、周期和深度；老式模拟信标不携带数据。请调谐到调幅信道。

Receive only. Fleet Broadcast is a 16 channel, 75 Bd multiplex sent as 1200 Bd differential BPSK from UHF military SATCOM satellites. Channel 0 is for synchronisation; the other channels are usually random-looking, encrypted data. This screen finds the carrier, the clock and the frame and shows what each channel looks like. Nothing is decoded and nothing is decrypted.	仅接收。舰队广播（Fleet Broadcast）是以 1200 Bd 差分 BPSK 从 UHF 军用卫星发送的 16 信道、75 Bd 复用。信道 0 用于同步；其他信道通常是随机外观的加密数据。此界面查找载波、时钟和帧，并显示每个信道的内容。不解码任何内容，也不解密任何内容。
sync pattern (repeats)	同步图案（重复）
reversals 1010...	反相 1010...
idle (constant)	空闲（恒定）
random-looking, probably encrypted: not decoded	呈随机外观，可能已加密：不解码
not enough data	数据不足
One row per frame, newest at the bottom, one column per channel (0 on the left); a filled cell is a 1 bit.	每帧一行，最新的在底部，每个信道一列（0 在左侧）；填充的单元格为 1 比特。
start the receiver to look for a Fleet Broadcast carrier	启动接收机以寻找舰队广播载波
Forget the log and the symbol count	清除日志和符号计数
no bits yet: waiting for a BPSK carrier with a 1200 Bd clock	尚无比特：正在等待带 1200 Bd 时钟的 BPSK 载波
Several columns of the 16-bit frame repeat, so the frame start cannot be told. The columns are shown as they come.	16 位帧的若干列重复出现，无法判断帧起点。各列按到达顺序显示。
Bits are flowing but no column repeats yet (the sync pattern of channel 0 is not published: a repeat of 3 to 64 frames is looked for). The columns are shown as they come.	比特在流动，但还没有列重复（信道 0 的同步图案未公开：会查找 3 至 64 帧的重复）。各列按到达顺序显示。
Col	列
Looks like	看起来像
Changes	变化
Ones	1 的个数
Last 64 bits (hex)	最近 64 比特（十六进制）
Frames as 16 bit words (hex, channel 0 is the top bit, newest last)	帧的 16 位字（十六进制，信道 0 为最高位，最新的在最后）
No plain text is expected here: the public description has never seen any on Fleet Broadcast. A random-looking channel is shown as hex and left alone.	这里预期不会有明文：公开资料从未见过舰队广播上有明文。呈随机外观的信道以十六进制显示，不做处理。

Last bits as received (the polarity is not published)	按接收顺序的最近比特（极性未公开）
What each part rests on:	各部分的依据：
Carriers 250.450, 250.550 and 250.650 MHz; 16 channel, 75 Bd multiplex; differential BPSK at 1200 Bd; channel 0 for synchronisation; the other channels usually random-looking, encrypted data, sometimes KG-84; no plain text ever seen: the description on r00t.cz (Fleet Broadcast Decoder v0.5, 2009).	载波 250.450、250.550 和 250.650 MHz；16 信道、75 Bd 复用；1200 Bd 差分 BPSK；信道 0 用于同步；其他信道通常是随机外观的加密数据，有时是 KG-84；从未见过明文：依据 r00t.cz 上的描述（Fleet Broadcast Decoder v0.5，2009）。
16 channels of 75 Bd make 1200 Bd: a frame is 16 bits, one bit per channel. A US Navy FLTSATCOM shore-terminal description also gives 75 bit/s subchannels sent time-division multiplexed at 1200 bit/s.	16 个 75 Bd 信道构成 1200 Bd：一帧 16 比特，每个信道 1 比特。一份美国海军 FLTSATCOM 岸基终端的描述还给出了以 1200 bit/s 时分复用发送的 75 bit/s 子信道。
The software on that page is personal-use, binary only: none of it is used. Everything here is written from the description.	该页面上的软件仅供个人使用，且只有二进制：这里未使用其中任何部分。所有内容均依据描述编写。
Not published, so not claimed: the sync pattern of channel 0, the pulse shape, the bit polarity, any message framing inside a channel, which satellite uses which carrier. The frame start is found as the one column of the 16-bit frame that repeats (3 to 64 frames) and taken for channel 0: that is an assumption, not a check against the real sync word. Use the polarity switch if the idle channels look swapped.	未公开，因此不作断言：信道 0 的同步图案、脉冲形状、比特极性、信道内部的报文成帧、哪颗卫星使用哪个载波。帧起点取 16 位帧中重复出现（3 至 64 帧）的那一列，并当作信道 0：这是一个假设，而非与真实同步字的核对。如果空闲信道看起来对调了，请使用极性开关。
Checked only on simulated signals built from the description (the test sync pattern is our own 31-bit sequence). No real recording was available. Nothing is decrypted and nothing is transmitted. Know your local rules before receiving.	仅依据描述构建的模拟信号验证过（测试同步图案是我们自己的 31 位序列）。没有真实录音可用。不解密任何内容，也不发射任何内容。接收前请了解当地法规。
Carriers (click to tune)	载波（点击调谐）
Bits	比特
The three Fleet Broadcast carriers listed on r00t.cz. The satellite's Doppler shift of a few kHz is searched around the tuned frequency.	r00t.cz 上列出的三个舰队广播载波。会在调谐频率附近搜索数 kHz 的卫星多普勒频移。
Invert bits	比特取反
The bit polarity is not published. Switch it when the idle channels look swapped.	比特极性未公开。空闲信道看起来对调时请切换。

Schumann transients, Jove and CALLISTO bursts: dB over the background. Whistlers: path score. Moon echo and beacon openings: sigma over the noise. Lower finds weaker events and more false ones.	舒曼瞬变、Jove 和 CALLISTO 爆发：高于背景的 dB。哨声：路径得分。月面回波和信标开通：高于噪声的 sigma。调低可发现更弱的事件，误报也更多。

One radio: control channel and calls together	单台电台：控制信道与通话兼顾
Start a P25, Motorola Type II, EDACS or MPT 1327 receiver on the control channel: the calls inside the capture are followed at the same time, with no second radio.	在控制信道上启动 P25、Motorola Type II、EDACS 或 MPT 1327 接收机：同时跟踪采集范围内的通话，无需第二台电台。
Following %d of %d voice slots from one capture: %.4f MHz, %.2f Msps	正在从同一段采集中跟踪 %d / %d 个语音时隙：%.4f MHz，%.2f Msps

Capture	采集
inside	在内
Retune to cover the control channel and the busiest channels	重新调谐以覆盖控制信道和最繁忙的信道
Moves the centre of the capture (and the sample rate) so the control channel and the most used voice channels are inside it, none of them on the DC spike at the centre. The control channel stays decoded.	移动采集的中心（和采样率），使控制信道和最常用的语音信道都落在其中，且都不在中心的直流尖峰上。控制信道保持解码状态。
Record each call	录制每次通话
Voice slots (calls at once)	语音时隙（同时通话数）
Each slot cuts its channel out of the capture and runs a decoder: more slots, more CPU. The DC spike is handled automatically; the app-wide Ignore DC spike switch is the manual one.	每个时隙从采集中切出自己的信道并运行解码器：时隙越多，CPU 占用越高。直流尖峰自动处理；全局的“忽略直流尖峰”开关是手动方式。
Calls followed from this capture	从此采集跟踪的通话

Slot	时隙
Input %.0f Hz, centre %.4f MHz. Traffic channels inside the capture are followed as FM; nothing encrypted is decoded.	输入 %.0f Hz，中心 %.4f MHz。采集范围内的业务信道按调频跟踪；不解码任何加密内容。
Radiosonde upload (SondeHub, APRS-IS)	探空仪上传（SondeHub、APRS-IS）
Sends every decoded radiosonde (position, speed, altitude, climb rate, temperature, humidity, pressure, frequency, type) to SondeHub and to APRS-IS sites such as wettersonde.net and radiosondy.info, after a station beacon with your location. Each target is off until you tick it.	把每个解码到的探空仪（位置、速度、高度、升速、温度、湿度、气压、频率、型号）在发送带位置的电台信标后，发送到 SondeHub 和 wettersonde.net、radiosondy.info 等 APRS-IS 网站。每个目标默认关闭，需勾选后启用。
Uploader callsign	上传者呼号
Antenna (optional)	天线（可选）

Privacy: the sites publish your station position and callsign. Set your location in View > Map first. Your passcode stays in your settings only. M10 and M20 sondes are not sent (no agreed APRS name for them).	隐私：这些网站会公开您的电台位置和呼号。请先在 视图 > 地图 中设置位置。您的密码只保存在设置中。M10 和 M20 探空仪不会发送（APRS 没有约定它们的名称）。

TV sound speakers	电视声音扬声器
Automatic plays 5.1 or 7.1 TV sound (Dolby Digital, Dolby Digital Plus, the bed of Dolby Atmos) on as many speakers as the output device has, and mixes it to stereo otherwise. Or always stereo, stereo with Dolby Surround (Lt/Rt) matrix for a surround decoder, 5.1 or 7.1.	“自动”按输出设备实际拥有的扬声器数量播放 5.1 或 7.1 电视声音（Dolby Digital、Dolby Digital Plus、Dolby Atmos 的基底），否则混为立体声。也可始终立体声、带 Dolby Surround（Lt/Rt）矩阵的立体声（供环绕声解码器使用）、5.1 或 7.1。
Pass Dolby audio through to my receiver (bitstream, experimental)	把杜比音频直通给我的接收机（码流，实验性）
Sends Dolby Digital and Dolby Digital Plus (with its Dolby Atmos) untouched over HDMI or optical to an AV receiver or soundbar, which decodes it: OnAir decodes nothing. Works only with devices that accept a bitstream; a laptop's own speakers cannot take it. Not tested on real hardware yet. The volume is then set on the receiver.	把 Dolby Digital 和 Dolby Digital Plus（含其中的 Dolby Atmos）原样通过 HDMI 或光纤发送给 AV 接收机或回音壁，由它来解码：OnAir 不做任何解码。仅适用于接受码流的设备；笔记本自带扬声器无法接收。尚未在真实硬件上测试。音量随后在接收机上调节。
Passthrough output	直通输出
The output that goes to the receiver. Empty: the TV sound's output. Linux: an ALSA device such as hdmi:CARD=0,DEV=0 (the receiver's HDMI port).	通向接收机的输出。留空：电视声音的输出。Linux：ALSA 设备，如 hdmi:CARD=0,DEV=0（接收机的 HDMI 端口）。

Dolby Digital (AC-3)	Dolby Digital (AC-3)
bed	基底

OnAir does not include, start or bundle ARDOP, VARA or Mercury. Each is a separate program that does the radio part; OnAir connects to it over TCP and carries the Winlink session through it.	OnAir 不包含、不启动也不捆绑 ARDOP、VARA 或 Mercury。它们各自是独立的程序，负责无线电部分；OnAir 通过 TCP 连接它们，并把 Winlink 会话承载于其上。
Tested only against mock modems built from the published command descriptions: no real ARDOP, VARA or Mercury modem was used.	仅依据公开的命令说明构建的模拟调制解调器测试过：未使用真实的 ARDOP、VARA 或 Mercury 调制解调器。

OnAir does not include, start or bundle this modem: it is a separate program that OnAir connects to over TCP.	OnAir 不包含、不启动也不捆绑此调制解调器：它是独立的程序，OnAir 通过 TCP 连接它。
The modem program owns the sound card and the radio's PTT. Set the audio device and PTT in the modem program, not in OnAir.	调制解调器程序独占声卡和电台的 PTT。请在调制解调器程序中设置音频设备和 PTT，而不是在 OnAir 中。

1. Install ardopcf (open source) and start it: ardopcf 8515 <capture device> <playback device> (see its USAGE files).	1. 安装 ardopcf（开源）并启动：ardopcf 8515 <采集设备> <播放设备>（见其 USAGE 文件）。
2. In OnAir add an ARDOP profile: host 127.0.0.1, command port 8515, data port 8516.	2. 在 OnAir 中添加 ARDOP 配置文件：主机 127.0.0.1，命令端口 8515，数据端口 8516。
3. Choose the BW (ARQBW), for example 500MAX, or leave it empty for the modem's own setting.	3. 选择 BW（ARQBW），例如 500MAX，或留空使用调制解调器自身的设置。
4. Add a gateway with link type External modem, pick the profile, enter the gateway's call sign.	4. 添加一个网关，链路类型选“外部调制解调器”，选择该配置文件，输入网关的呼号。
ardopcf listens on all network interfaces by default: keep the ports behind a firewall.	ardopcf 默认监听所有网络接口：请把端口置于防火墙之后。
ardopcf is open source (MIT licence). ARDOP's specification is public.	ardopcf 是开源软件（MIT 许可证）。ARDOP 的规范是公开的。
1. Install VARA HF from its author's site (EA5HVK) and start it. It is Windows software.	1. 从作者网站（EA5HVK）安装 VARA HF 并启动。它是 Windows 软件。
2. Linux and macOS: people run it under Wine in its own prefix (see the Pat wiki page on VARA and the DF3FY guide, linked in docs/HFMODEMS-3.9.md). OnAir cannot check that setup.	2. Linux 和 macOS：人们用 Wine 在独立前缀中运行它（见 Pat 维基上关于 VARA 的页面和 DF3FY 指南，链接见 docs/HFMODEMS-3.9.md）。OnAir 无法检查该配置。
3. In OnAir add a VARA HF profile: host 127.0.0.1, command port 8300, data port 8301 (the defaults; check VARA's Settings > TCP ports).	3. 在 OnAir 中添加 VARA HF 配置文件：主机 127.0.0.1，命令端口 8300，数据端口 8301（默认值；请核对 VARA 的“设置 > TCP 端口”）。
4. BW: 500, 2300 (the default) or 2750. Add a gateway with link type External modem and pick the profile.	4. BW：500、2300（默认）或 2750。添加网关，链路类型选“外部调制解调器”，并选择该配置文件。
5. For a peer-to-peer session mark the gateway entry as another station: OnAir then sends P2P SESSION.	5. 对等会话请把网关条目标记为另一电台：OnAir 会发送 P2P SESSION。
VARA's free version is speed limited; a licence key (bought from the author) removes the limit. OnAir does not read or change it.	VARA 的免费版有速度限制；从作者处购买的许可证密钥可解除限制。OnAir 不读取也不更改它。
VARA is closed, proprietary, Windows-only software by EA5HVK. Its licence and any registration are between you and its author. OnAir does not contain or decode VARA.	VARA 是 EA5HVK 开发的闭源专有软件，仅限 Windows。其许可和注册是您与作者之间的事。OnAir 不包含也不解码 VARA。
1. Install VARA FM from its author's site and start it. It is Windows software.	1. 从作者网站安装 VARA FM 并启动。它是 Windows 软件。
2. In OnAir add a VARA FM profile: host 127.0.0.1, command port 8300, data port 8301 (VARA FM and HF both default to these, so run one at a time or change one program's ports).	2. 在 OnAir 中添加 VARA FM 配置文件：主机 127.0.0.1，命令端口 8300，数据端口 8301（VARA FM 和 HF 都默认使用这些端口，因此同时只运行一个，或修改其中一个程序的端口）。
3. VARA FM has no BW command. Digipeaters go in the gateway's Via field and are sent as: CONNECT <mycall> <call> via <digi1> <digi2>.	3. VARA FM 没有 BW 命令。数字中继填入网关的 Via 字段，发送格式为：CONNECT <我的呼号> <对方呼号> via <中继1> <中继2>。
4. Add a gateway with link type External modem and pick the profile.	4. 添加网关，链路类型选“外部调制解调器”，并选择该配置文件。
VARA FM is closed, proprietary, Windows-only software by EA5HVK. OnAir does not contain or decode it.	VARA FM 是 EA5HVK 开发的闭源专有软件，仅限 Windows。OnAir 不包含也不解码它。
1. Install Mercury (Rhizomatica, open source) and start it; it exposes a VARA-style TCP interface.	1. 安装 Mercury（Rhizomatica，开源）并启动；它提供 VARA 风格的 TCP 接口。
2. In OnAir add a Mercury profile: host 127.0.0.1, command port 8300, data port 8301 (Mercury: -p <base_port>, data = base + 1).	2. 在 OnAir 中添加 Mercury 配置文件：主机 127.0.0.1，命令端口 8300，数据端口 8301（Mercury：-p <基础端口>，数据端口 = 基础端口 + 1）。
3. BW: 500, 2300 or 2750 (2750 behaves as 2300 today). Add a gateway with link type External modem and pick the profile.	3. BW：500、2300 或 2750（目前 2750 的表现与 2300 相同）。添加网关，链路类型选“外部调制解调器”，并选择该配置文件。
A Mercury call can last up to 300 s before it gives up: the connect timeout of the profile is 330 s unless you set one.	Mercury 呼叫最长可持续 300 秒才放弃：除非您另行设置，该配置文件的连接超时为 330 秒。

What leaves this computer	哪些数据会离开这台电脑
The update check (when on) sends one HTTPS request to api.github.com, or to your mirror, with the header User-Agent: OnAir/<version>. GitHub therefore sees your IP address and the app version, nothing else. The plug-in Store (View > Plugins > Store) contacts its registry only when you press Refresh, open a plug-in's page or install one: HTTPS requests to the registry address with the header User-Agent: OnAir-Store/<version>; that server sees your IP address and the app version, nothing else. Sending your station to the map server and the Discord status are opt-in and off until you turn them on. The HFDL live ground-station list (below) is off until you turn it on.	更新检查（开启时）向 api.github.com 或您的镜像发送一次 HTTPS 请求，请求头为 User-Agent: OnAir/<version>。因此 GitHub 会看到您的 IP 地址和应用版本，仅此而已。插件商店（视图 > 插件 > 商店）仅在您按“刷新”、打开插件页面或安装插件时联系其注册表：向注册表地址发送 HTTPS 请求，请求头 User-Agent: OnAir-Store/<version>；该服务器会看到您的 IP 地址和应用版本，仅此而已。向地图服务器发送电台位置和 Discord 状态均为自主选择，开启前不会发送。HFDL 实时地面站列表（下方）同理。

Averages 40 spectrum frames of the whole capture and lists every carrier: centre, width, symbol rate (snapped to a standard rate) and C/N. Widen the capture to cover the LNB band.	对整段采集的 40 帧频谱取平均，并列出每个载波：中心、宽度、符号率（对齐到标准速率）和 C/N。加宽采集以覆盖 LNB 频段。

Best first: a high mean SNR that stays steady and is decoded in every reading. Readings are of the ensemble the service belongs to, so ranking compares services across the ensembles you visit.	排名原则：平均 SNR 高、稳定、且每次读数都能解码。读数属于服务所在的节目集，因此排名跨您收听到的各个节目集比较服务。

Built from the decodes of this session. Heard: callsigns in the messages, newest first. Band summary: spots in the last 30 minutes per band, with the farthest one and the directions it is open to (needs your position and the stations' locators). Clear frequency: the widest gap in 200 - 2900 Hz for the even and the odd slot, from the last decodes: call there to avoid sitting on somebody. Suggested band: the busiest of the last hour, every band still revisited every 30 minutes.	由本次会话的解码构建。已听到：消息中的呼号，最新的在前。波段摘要：最近 30 分钟各波段的 spot 数，及最远台和波段开通的方向（需要您的位置和电台的网格定位）。空闲频率：根据最近解码，偶数与奇数时隙在 200 - 2900 Hz 中最宽的间隙：在那里呼叫可避免压到别人。建议波段：最近一小时最繁忙的波段，每个波段仍每 30 分钟回访一次。
Burst finder	突发查找器
Bursts of the same beacon ID grouped. A real beacon repeats about every 50 seconds and, if it carries a position, sends it again; a 5 second echo or a single burst is not flagged. Self-test bursts are counted separately. Receive only: do not rely on this for safety.	按相同信标 ID 分组的突发。真实信标约每 50 秒重复一次，且若带有位置会再次发送；5 秒的回波或单个突发不会被标记。自检突发单独计数。仅接收：请勿据此判断安全。

Line code worked out from the pulse and gap lengths of this burst: how the bits are coded, the short and long length, the gap that separates repeated rows and the bits per row. The flex line can be pasted into rtl_433. Noise bursts are not claimed; the confidence tells how well the lengths fit.	由该突发的脉冲与间隔长度推断线路编码：比特如何编码、短与长长度、分隔重复行的间隔以及每行比特数。flex 行可粘贴到 rtl_433。噪声突发不作断言；置信度表示长度拟合的程度。

The tones heard in the last 20 seconds are matched against CTCSS, DTMF, the five-tone plans (ZVEI, CCIR, EEA, EIA, DZVEI) and SELCAL. It is a guess to pick the right decoder, not a lock. Dialled keys are joined into numbers and codes; a repeat within 2 minutes is counted, not listed.	最近 20 秒听到的音调与 CTCSS、DTMF、五音制式（ZVEI、CCIR、EEA、EIA、DZVEI）和 SELCAL 匹配。这是选择正确解码器的猜测，而非锁定。拨出的键会拼成数字与代码；2 分钟内的重复会计数，不列出。

LNB drift watch	LNB 漂移监测
Tune onto a steady beacon or carrier and leave the receiver locked: the offset of the carrier from the tuned frequency is the LNB's error. It shows the error in kHz and ppm of the LO, the drift per minute, and says when it has settled (an LNB drifts while it warms up: wait before fine pointing).	调谐到一个稳定的信标或载波并保持锁定：载波与调谐频率的偏差就是 LNB 的误差。它以 kHz 和 LO 的 ppm 显示误差、每分钟漂移量，并告知何时已稳定（LNB 在预热期间会漂移：精细指向前请等待）。
LNB LO (MHz)	LNB LO (MHz)

settled	已稳定
still drifting	仍在漂移
ATIS letter tracker and VOLMET	ATIS 字母跟踪与 VOLMET
Reads the live transcripts of the airband channels (Transcripts need the speech model). A channel whose label says ATIS or VOLMET, or that read an information letter twice, is an ATIS channel: the current letter, its age, and a notice when the letter changes. A VOLMET read-out lists the airports it named.	读取航空频段信道的实时转录（转录需要语音模型）。标签写着 ATIS 或 VOLMET、或两次读出信息字母的信道即 ATIS 信道：显示当前字母、其更新时间和字母变更提示。VOLMET 播报会列出其中提到的机场。

VOLMET airports	VOLMET 机场
Constellation check: no regular grid recognised.	星座图检查：未识别出规则网格。
The symbol cloud looks like	符号云看起来像
A check of the received symbols against each QAM grid, judged against what a random cloud would score on the same grid (a finer grid always fits better). The decoder finds its order from the stream by itself; this is a second opinion.	将接收到的符号与各种 QAM 网格逐一核对，并与同一网格上随机符号云的得分比较（更细的网格总是拟合得更好）。解码器自行从码流中确定阶数；这是第二意见。
decoder uses	解码器使用
Digital sideband levels	数字边带电平
Power of the lower and upper digital sideband against the host signal, from the capture spectrum. A first-adjacent station hurts one side: a clear imbalance tells which. Needs a capture at least 440 kHz wide around the station.	从采集频谱看载波上下数字边带相对于主信号的功率。相邻频道强台会损害其中一侧：明显的不平衡可判断是哪一侧。需要至少 440 kHz 宽的采集。
The capture is too narrow around the station (needs +-240 kHz, bins under 2 kHz).	电台周围的采集太窄（需要 ±240 kHz，频点小于 2 kHz）。
No HD Radio sidebands found in the spectrum.	频谱中未找到 HD Radio 边带。
lower	下
upper	上
imbalance	不平衡
Free channels for	空闲信道，按
pilots	导频
Channels that nobody is using (from the last scan), spaced so the pilots do not clash: not within 20 MHz of a transmitter, at least 37 MHz apart. Channels never measured are not offered: scan first.	无人使用的信道（来自上次扫描），间隔设计为避免导频冲突：距发射台不小于 20 MHz，彼此至少相隔 37 MHz。从未测量过的信道不提供：请先扫描。
Scan first.	请先扫描。
No free channel found.	未找到空闲信道。

Click Set, then press the key (with Ctrl, Shift or Alt if you like) or the middle / side mouse button. Esc cancels. Every action can have two keys.	点击“设置”，然后按下按键（可搭配 Ctrl、Shift 或 Alt）或鼠标中键 / 侧键。Esc 取消。每个动作可设两个键。

Not available for rebinding: the left and right mouse buttons, Esc and Tab. A key of the TV picture or the FT8 screen wins over a global key while that screen is shown.	不可重绑定：鼠标左键和右键、Esc 和 Tab。电视画面或 FT8 界面上的按键在对应界面显示时优先于全局按键。
The step is used by Tune by the step on the wheel and by the tune up / down and step keys above.	该步进用于滚轮的“按步调谐”，以及上方的调谐增/减和步进键。

Every key and the wheel go back to the defaults. Continue?	所有按键和滚轮都将恢复为默认。继续吗？

Notifications	通知
Performance	性能
Profiles	配置文件
Start-up mode	启动模式
The mode OnAir opens with: the one you had last (default) or always the same one.	OnAir 启动时打开的模式：上次使用的（默认）或固定一个。
Start-up frequency	启动频率
The frequency OnAir starts on: the last one (default) or a fixed frequency below.	OnAir 启动时的频率：上次使用的（默认）或下方固定的频率。
Fixed start-up frequency (MHz)	固定启动频率 (MHz)
Used when Start-up frequency is A fixed frequency.	当启动频率选择“固定频率”时使用。
Start the receiver when OnAir starts	OnAir 启动时启动接收机
Opens the radio you used last and starts receiving at once. Off (default): press Start.	打开上次使用的电台并立即开始接收。关闭（默认）：按“开始”。
After a crash, restore the last session	崩溃后恢复上次会话
When OnAir did not end normally: ask (default), restore it at once, or never offer it.	OnAir 未正常退出时：询问（默认）、立即恢复或不再提示。
Confirm before quitting	退出前确认
Ask before the window closes: never (default), only while the radio receives or records, or always.	窗口关闭前询问：从不（默认）、仅在接收或录制时、或总是。
Aircraft units	航空单位
ADS-B, Mode A/C and HFDL: aviation units (knots, feet, nautical miles) or metric (km/h or m/s, metres, km). Only what is shown and exported changes; the feeds stay in aviation units.	ADS-B、A/C 模式和 HFDL：航空单位（节、英尺、海里）或公制（km/h 或 m/s、米、km）。只有显示和导出的单位改变；数据推送仍使用航空单位。
Metric speed	公制速度
km/h or m/s, when the aircraft units are metric.	航空单位为公制时的速度单位：km/h 或 m/s。
FFT size	FFT 大小
Bins of the spectrum. Automatic picks 8192 to 32768 from the sample rate; more bins show finer detail and cost more CPU.	频谱的频点数。自动按采样率在 8192 至 32768 之间选择；频点越多细节越细，CPU 占用越高。
Waterfall speed	瀑布图速度
Rows per second. Every frame (default) is about 30; slower rows each keep the highest level of the frames in between.	每秒行数。“每帧”（默认）约 30 行；较慢的每行取帧之间的最高电平。
Max-hold line	最大保持线
A second line that keeps the highest level, falling at the rate above.	第二条保持最高电平的线，按上方速率下降。
Min-hold line	最小保持线
A line under the signals at the lowest level (the noise floor).	位于信号下方最低电平处的线（底噪）。
Noise floor line	底噪线
A line at the noise floor with its level.	一条标出底噪电平的线。
Station names on the spectrum	频谱上的电台名称
Names from the band plan, the frequency database, FM, DAB, DVB and the memories on the spectrum and the waterfall.	来自频段规划、频率数据库、FM、DAB、DVB 和记忆的名称，显示在频谱和瀑布图上。
Spectrum line colour	频谱线颜色
The colour of the spectrum line. The colour-blind safe ones read for everybody.	频谱线的颜色。色盲安全配色对所有人都清晰。
Max-hold line colour	最大保持线颜色
The usual grey, or one of the line colours.	通常的灰色，或线条颜色之一。
Smooth the spectrum line	平滑频谱线
Mild smoothing passes over the line; As the look keeps the look's own choice.	对线条做轻度平滑；“随外观”保持该外观自己的选择。
Frequency scale	频率刻度
Frequencies, or offsets from the tuned frequency.	频率，或相对调谐频率的偏移。
Scale text size	刻度文字大小
Size of the scale numbers, times the look's own.	刻度数字的大小，为该外观自身尺寸的倍数。
Smooth zoom	平滑缩放
The wheel zoom eases in over a few frames. Off: it jumps.	滚轮缩放在几帧内平滑过渡。关闭：直接跳转。
Waterfall floor below the noise (dB)	瀑布图底噪下限 (dB)
With the range following the noise floor: how far under the noise the darkest colour sits.	范围跟随底噪时：最暗颜色位于底噪下方多远。
Waterfall range (dB)	瀑布图范围 (dB)
With the range following the noise floor: from the darkest to the brightest colour.	范围跟随底噪时：从最暗到最亮颜色的区间。
Default range: bottom (dB)	默认范围：下限 (dB)
Where the spectrum's scale starts after Reset and the range key (Ctrl+Home by default).	按“重置”和范围键（默认 Ctrl+Home）后频谱刻度的起点。
Default range: top (dB)	默认范围：上限 (dB)
Where the spectrum's scale ends after Reset and the range key.	按“重置”和范围键后频谱刻度的终点。
Range keys step (dB)	范围键步进 (dB)
How far the range keys (Ctrl+Up and Ctrl+Down by default) move the scale.	范围键（默认 Ctrl+Up 和 Ctrl+Down）移动刻度的幅度。
Automatic gain	自动增益
The radio's gain is set by itself (Auto in the gain control); off: the gains you set.	增益由电台自行设置（增益控制中的“自动”）；关闭：使用您设置的增益。
Remove the DC spike	去除直流尖峰
Takes the centre spike of the radio out of the samples.	把电台的中心尖峰从采样中移除。
Correct IQ imbalance	校正 IQ 不平衡
Removes the mirror image a radio's IQ imbalance makes.	消除电台 IQ 不平衡产生的镜像。
Tune off-centre by itself	自动偏移调谐
Keeps the wanted signal away from the radio's centre spike.	让有用信号避开电台的中心尖峰。
Notch fixed spurs	陷波固定杂散
Finds carriers that never move (the radio's own) and removes them.	找出从不移动的载波（电台自身的）并将其移除。
Noise blanker	噪声消隐器
Cuts short pulses (ignition, power lines).	切除短脉冲（点火、电力线干扰）。
Noise blanker threshold	噪声消隐器阈值
Times the mean level that counts as a pulse. Lower cuts more.	视为脉冲的均值倍数。越低切得越多。
Noise blanker width (us)	噪声消隐器宽度 (µs)
How long each cut is.	每次切除的时长。
Output device	输出设备
The sound output for every mode that has not picked its own in its Audio pop-up. Default: the system's.	每个未在“音频”弹出菜单中自行选择输出设备的模式的默认声音输出。默认：系统输出。
Audio AGC for new modes	新模式的音频 AGC
The audio level control a mode starts with the first time you use it. Each mode keeps its own after that.	首次使用某模式时的音频电平控制。之后每个模式保留自己的设置。
Squelch for new modes	新模式的静噪
The squelch a mode starts with the first time you use it: off, by level, or by speech-to-noise.	首次使用某模式时的静噪：关闭、按电平或按语音噪声比。
Squelch opens at (dB)	静噪打开阈值 (dB)
Level squelch: dBFS. Speech to noise: dB.	电平静噪：dBFS。语音噪声比：dB。
FM bass (dB)	FM 低音 (dB)
Tone control of FM broadcast.	FM 广播的音调控制。
FM treble (dB)	FM 高音 (dB)
FM warm sound	FM 温暖音色
A gentle -3 dB above 6 kHz.	6 kHz 以上温和衰减 -3 dB。
FM audio bandwidth	FM 音频带宽
Full is 15 kHz; narrower is quieter on a weak station.	满带宽为 15 kHz；较窄的带宽在弱台上更安静。
Hide short drop-outs	隐藏短暂中断
Fills short gaps in TV and DAB sound instead of a click.	填补电视和 DAB 声音中的短暂间隙，而不是发出咔哒声。
Sound card rate (Hz)	声卡速率 (Hz)
The sample rate of the sound card input and output.	声卡输入和输出的采样率。
IQ file name	IQ 文件名
Tokens: {mode} {freq} (MHz) {khz} {rate} (Msps) {date} {time} {utcdate} {utctime}. Empty: onair_{mode}_{freq}MHz_{rate}Msps_{date}-{time}. Keep {freq}MHz and {rate}Msps so OnAir can read the file back.	占位符：{mode} {freq} (MHz) {khz} {rate} (Msps) {date} {time} {utcdate} {utctime}。留空：onair_{mode}_{freq}MHz_{rate}Msps_{date}-{time}。请保留 {freq}MHz 和 {rate}Msps，以便 OnAir 读回文件。
Split IQ recordings every (MB)	每 (MB) 分割 IQ 录制
Starts a new file when one reaches this size. 0: one file.	文件达到此大小时新建文件。0：单个文件。
Stop recording below free space (MB)	剩余空间低于 (MB) 时停止录制
An IQ recording stops when the disk has less than this left. 0: only when it is full.	磁盘剩余空间少于此值时停止 IQ 录制。0：仅在磁盘写满时停止。
SigMF metadata beside IQ files	IQ 文件旁的 SigMF 元数据
A .sigmf-meta file with the frequency, rate and mode next to each recording.	每个录制旁保存一个含频率、采样率和模式的 .sigmf-meta 文件。
Audio recording format	音频录制格式
WAV, FLAC or MP3 (MP3 needs the LAME library).	WAV、FLAC 或 MP3（MP3 需要 LAME 库）。
Record audio only while there is sound	仅在有声时录制音频
Leaves the silence out.	跳过静音部分。
One audio file per transmission	每次发射一个音频文件
A new file each time the sound starts again.	每次声音重新开始时新建文件。
Keep recording audio across retunes	换频时继续录制音频
Off: a retune ends the file.	关闭：换频会结束当前文件。
TV pause buffer (MB)	电视暂停缓冲区 (MB)
How much of the TV service is kept so Pause can go on later.	为稍后继续播放而保留的电视节目缓冲量。
Keys, mouse buttons and wheel	按键、鼠标按钮与滚轮
Every action with its keys: Set records a key, Clear removes it, Default puts it back. Conflicts are marked; import and export the whole set.	每个动作及其按键：“设置”录制按键，“清除”移除，“默认”恢复。冲突会被标出；可导入导出整套设置。
System notifications	系统通知
Alerts (watch lists, automations, pass reminders) also go to the system's notification centre.	提醒（监视列表、自动化、过境提醒）也会发送到系统通知中心。
Banner in OnAir	OnAir 内横幅
Alerts show a banner at the top of the OnAir window.	提醒会在 OnAir 窗口顶部显示横幅。
Banner time (seconds)	横幅显示时长（秒）
How long the banner stays.	横幅停留的时长。
Alert sounds	提醒声音
Alerts that play a sound play it. Off: silent.	会播放声音的提醒将播放声音。关闭：静音。
Quiet hours	静默时段
No system notifications and no alert sounds between the two times below (the banner still shows).	下方两个时间之间不发送系统通知、不播放提醒声音（横幅仍显示）。
Quiet from (minutes after midnight)	静默开始（午夜后分钟数）
e.g. 1320 is 22:00. Local time.	例如 1320 表示 22:00。本地时间。
Quiet until (minutes after midnight)	静默结束（午夜后分钟数）
e.g. 420 is 07:00. Local time.	例如 420 表示 07:00。本地时间。
Map tile server	地图瓦片服务器
Address of the map tiles with {z} {x} {y}. Empty: OpenStreetMap (https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png). Tiles of another server are cached apart. The server sees which map area you look at.	地图瓦片地址，含 {z} {x} {y}。留空：OpenStreetMap (https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png）。其他服务器的瓦片单独缓存。服务器会看到您查看的地图区域。
Map tile cache size (MB)	地图瓦片缓存大小 (MB)
When the tiles take more room, the ones used longest ago are deleted.	瓦片占用超过此空间大小时，最久未用的瓦片会被删除。
Keep map tiles between runs	在运行之间保留地图瓦片
Off: the tiles of a session are deleted when OnAir ends.	关闭：OnAir 退出时删除本次会话的瓦片。
Fetch online map tiles	获取在线地图瓦片
The map screens load OpenStreetMap tiles (or your tile server's). Only tile numbers are sent; the server sees your IP address and the area. Off: offline maps only.	地图界面加载 OpenStreetMap 瓦片（或您的瓦片服务器）。只发送瓦片编号；服务器会看到您的 IP 地址和查看区域。关闭：仅使用离线地图。
DVB error correction on	DVB 纠错开启
CPU, GPU or Automatic (default) for the DVB-T2 LDPC decoder.	DVB-T2 LDPC 解码器使用 CPU、GPU 或自动（默认）。
Settings profiles	设置配置文件
Save every setting of this window and the keys under a name, switch between them (e.g. Home, Portable, Night), and export or import a profile as a file.	将此窗口的所有设置和按键保存为一个名称，在它们之间切换（如家用、便携、夜间），并可把配置文件导出或导入为文件。
Verbose logging	详细日志
USB transfer counts, data rates, stall restarts and frame times in the log. For bug reports.	在日志中记录 USB 传输计数、数据速率、停滞重启和帧时间。用于问题报告。
Current profile:	当前配置文件：

The language to use where a channel offers several:\nprogramme names and descriptions in the guide, the audio track and the subtitles.\nWhere a channel does not have it, what it sends first is used.	频道提供多种语言时使用的语言：\n节目指南中的节目名称和说明、音轨和字幕。\n频道没有该语言时，使用其首先发送的语言。
This is a small preview. For the big picture, open the TV tab (the pop-out window and full screen are there too).	这是一个小预览。要看大画面，请打开“电视”标签页（弹出窗口和全屏也在那里）。
This multiplex carries several physical layer pipes. The receiver decodes one at a time.	此复用包含多个物理层管道。接收机一次解码一个。
This service's sound is AC-4 (Dolby). OnAir needs an optional FFmpeg with an AC-4 decoder to play it.	此节目的声音为 AC-4（杜比）。OnAir 需要带有 AC-4 解码器的可选 FFmpeg 才能播放。
UTC	UTC
Use the graphics chip to decode the video. It saves processor time for the receiver.\nSwitch it off if the picture shows green or garbled patches (a graphics driver problem).	使用显卡芯片解码视频，可为接收机节省处理器时间。\n如果画面出现绿色或花屏色块（显卡驱动问题），请关闭它。
When a fade swallows part of the stream or damages the pictures after it, generate the missing pictures\n(Apple ML interpolation) instead of freezing or showing smears. It hides the loss; it cannot bring the data back.\nThe sound is not changed: a dropout stays a clean silence.	当衰落吞掉部分码流或损坏其后的画面时，生成缺失的画面\n（Apple ML 插帧），而不是画面冻结或出现拖影。这只是掩盖损失，无法找回数据。\n声音不变：中断时仍是干净的静音。
Write the raw audio stream of this service (DRA, else MPEG audio) to a file you can play with FFmpeg (see the README).	将此节目的原始音频流（DRA，否则为 MPEG 音频）写入文件，可用 FFmpeg 播放（见 README）。
